<6.1r> Page Image6   Ms. Var. 259 6   1   Nortons Ordinall    Chap 4 If the grosse work with all its circumstance were done in 3 years   it were a blessed chance. Which once don is done for ever.   Chap 5  Aristotle  said compound that the stone equall that in him repugnance be none   Tasting is perillous while the stone is working till perfect red bee made. A   servant tasted of the white stone & was soddenly struck with a strong paralysy whom my   Master cured with Bezoars of the mine. Yet some parts seperable may bee tasted   for tryalls sake before conjunctions to know whether they bee well wrought or not   Of liquors in our work one is called by  Plato  stilla roris madidi, by  Democritus    water permanent, by  Pythagoras  aqua vitae, another said water of Lytharg with water   of Arock 6112r12  would make lac virginis,  Aristotle  calls it quintessence. There's another liquor   fresher then well water in tast, yet will never wast, Though it bee occupied evermore   it will never bee lesse in store This  Democritus  calld Lux umbra carens, water Orient  Hermes  said noe liquor is so necessary as water of crude mercury.   The ancients put 9 circulations, moderns use but seaven. Our circulation is   from fire on high which endeth with water contrary. Another circulation beginneth   with air & endeth with its contrary cleane earth & faire. From fire to earth   thence to water thenc to ayre, & thence by a meane passing to earth, & thence   to fire, such circulations the red work doth desire, & others bee better for the white   Some circulations are long lasting 30 weeks others shorter, & one with another 
  make 26 weeks apeece   Chap 7 decoction is in a heat that will scald pig or goose, to make our Letharge   sweate. Such heat as dryeth lawn kercheifs fair in 30 operations serveth for our   Ayre. But for divisions use such heat as Cooks make when they roast grosse meat,   The same heat (videlicet of roasting) with a circular fire for seperation of dividents we desire   (For circulation use ignis candens which must bee continuall & invisible. Ignis humidus   dissevers matter cleaving to the vessel sides & makes thick things thin. Magnesia is fire   full of perills & illusion to the master as well as the worke therefore fetch breath   from your foot defending your mouth eyes nose & eares from the fumes which hath  prejudiced some unwary. Theres fire of desiccation for matters imbibed with moysture   Ignis corrodens departs the elements) Theres a heat of coaction for mineralls of hard   liquefaction which cannot bee too strong nor too long. A heat of calcination of foul   metalls which may not burn nor melt them least they bee spoyled. Theres a heat to sublime   spirits. & a heat for projection. Of fire one point of excesse spoiles all & one point   too little is insufficient. There is noe better nor other guide in this but sight   & experience.   Hee had the red stone stoln by a Merchants wife. Another stole the elixir of life   etc. Hee intimates how few obtein the red & says of  Albertus magnus  the black frier &   Frier  Bacon  that of the red stone they had noe consideration It to increase in   multiplication. Dastins dreame    Stanza 3. This worke dedicated to  Apollo  & the 7 Gods   Stanza 4. Of this matter (☿ ) above between starrs seaven by Gods & Goddesses all   of one assent was sent caducifer to earth from heaven: Saturnus as Bedell to summon   a generall assembly to make up a match between two of  Juppiter s royall line.  Mercury  rules all aswages revives dissolve & reconciles. <6.1v> Page Image2    Stanza 10. Joyn kind with kind.  Diana  &  Venus  in marriage be notable.   Stanz 14 A mighty king had certain brethren impure leprous & skabbd, hee advised   one should voluntarily bleed to death that the rest might be clensed by his blood, but they   wept & cryd they were not able to bleed & that their blood was infected. Then went   they to their Mother ☿  & shee said the king must dy for them & bee made young again   as  Aeson  was by  Medea s potions. The king full sad consents.   Stanz 34 The king choses the bright moon at full to be first his   mother & then his wife. And at this feast were all the Gods   turne from black to   white. Juppiter lets his mantle fall cloathed in lillys.  Mars  forgets his sturdy black  hardinesse & casts of his old rusty Habergeon.  Venus  forsook her minerall rednesse, Took   Gold for greek   Stanz 39 The king (to bee anew conceived & borne enters the marriage bed where   he lost his colour & dyed in the maydens sight the serpent sheds out his poyson & the   Queen & mayds for feare  fledd  ascending up & down 7 times. The king revivs &   clenses his brethren. S   Stanz 43 First appears the ravens bill & that after turns white. The kings birth   approaches. Of nine virgins hee devoured three The six for feare flew forth   into the aire   Stanz 45 Hee drank so much well water that his six sisters made noe resistance but   would have devoured. The black monkes Earth of Earth.    Hee bids doe earth of earth with water of the wood together 3 days & then depart them   & there shall bee a water shining & earth heavy as metall. Distill that earth three days   in green hue. Distill in a lymbeck first a cleer water, then in a stronger heate a   fire redd as blood, & an earth shall remain, which must in purgatory bee made brighter   then the sun, & that in 3 howers. Then doe her in a cleane glass again & again   with her water till shee hath drunk it, then give her her blood. Then feed her   with milk & meat & make projection.   Our Gold & silver are not common but come all of one image but water of   the wood maketh the marriage   Arsnick sublimed, with ☿  calcined 9 parts, & grownd together with water of might   all anon runneth to pure bright ☿ , & then on the fire fix together, & must bee   fed with meat & milk till they bee strong.   Hee commends the cleer water whitening, the oyle rubifying, & the earth that is   first black then red, & all this in 3 howers. The earth shall turn red & then   the red oyle to him must goe, red ferment, & red ☿  also & grow together 7   weeks. Then is it a medicine for metalls & for man. The hunting of the Green Lyon    When thow hast fed the Lyon with ☉  & ☽  continue an easy heat till they   kisse & thence draw the Lyons blood to feed the king when hee is risen from   the dead. But that will bee a long while first, ere you see him black &   dead. Continue an easy heat till you see him separate from his vile vituperate earth   which will bee black & light like a fusball. Your magnet in the midst will bee faire   & white, which when you see augment the heat till the matter grow dry. Then without   delay take away the excrements, & prepare a pure bright bed to lodg this young   child in, wherin let him ly alone till hee be throughly dry, Then after such drought   give him drink. ____ when your matter is (as before) fit, before you shut the glasse <6.2r> Page Image3  give it som of the Lyons sweat & grind them well together till they bee fast & fly   no whither, then seal up the glasse & put it in a furnace to dry the matter, then imbibe   again. But bee sure the matter bee well dryed first that the drown it not or  prolong the work by too much moysture, yet let it not bee over dry least it dy for   thirst. Thus imbibe six times warily, eight days passing twixt ilk day of the six   to dry up the moist & make it fixt. At the ninth time seale thy glasse & let it   stand 6 weeks with such a heat that blacknes past hee may grow white. The seventh week   let him rest till thou ferment, unless thou will continue the heat to redd.   For white ferment worke pure ☽  alone with the blood of green Lyon as thou didst in   the begining (making one thing of 3) till the magnet bee white & soe work with gold for the red   set in thy ferments white & red severally the same however (videlicet with the 🜍 s ) that they may bee ready   to feed the king. Your ferment one fourth part must bee unto your magnet made evenly   & joyne them warme & not cold, put them warme into thy glasse then seal it up as   it was. And circulate till it bee black white & red.   The best multiplication is by continuall fermentation. Have therefore always   ferment in readynesse. Anonymi pag 409    Your stone divide in parts three & the fourth of the ferment must bee.   Divide the matters equally one 1/2 for white the other for red & when to each their   propper ferments are put seal up the glasse & putrefy etc After  Pearce the black Monck s terra terrae in some coppys    Take wind & water white & greene & thence draw a lac virgine, which some   call a water clear, then make the fier stronger when the white fume doth   appeare; Chang your receiver & continue longer: And then shall com a   fire red as blood & full of ire. Quod dicitur menstruum faetus, sol  nostrum    in quo fit dissolutio, congelatio, sublimatio, fixatio in quo fit 🜍 is  nostri sive   foliati creatio. Ripleys Epistle to K. Edward the Fourth    Stanz 8. Metalls as well as all other things are multiplyable in their kind   Stanz 10.  Aristotle  in his meteors saith metalls are not transmutable, but adds   this word of comfort, unles they bee reduced to their first matter.   Stanz 11. He treateth also of divers 🜍 s  but especially of 2 & of 2 ☿ is    white & redd, yet all of one root our Lead which was long before I understood it   Stanz 13 Joyn kind with kind, joyne the Elements that they strive not, make the   quadrangle a circle  14 Turn earth to water & water to wind & thereof make fire but beware    of  Noah s flood. 17 eat drink & sweat temperatly & do all things by physicall rules   walking by rivers & fountains after meat & on the morrow up hills yet not so high   but that you may easily descend. purg gros humors by sweat & dry you with   a napkin etc. 25 first calcine then putrefy dissolve distill sublime descend & fix  our  Riplys 12 Gates. wrote 1471. From 1450 hee erred to 1470, & dyed 1490.    Praeface Our stone is ☿  but not vulgar & it is nigher in somthings then in   Others. Wee have 3 ☿ s  or menstrues (as  Raymund  calls them) of which two are  superficiall, & the third essentiall to the sun & moon [i.e. their 🜍  dissolvd in form of oyle which is   their essentiall matter. therefore hee calleth] mercury of metalls essentiall, & the materiall principle of the stone <6.2v> Page Image4  In Sun & moon our menstrue is not seene [the matter being a dry calx] it appeareth   but by effect to sight, we meane the stone a soul most bright (i.e. it appears only in   the augmentation of the stones vertue. or the third menstrue our stone appeares not to bee   a menstrue but by its effect as resolving to a liquor in new decoction. etc When tis calld ☿  as well as 🜍 . Se Spagnet ] the whole compound is called our lead   With the first menstrue wee calcine perfect bodys but none uncleane enters but   the Green Lyon which mediats twixt the Sun & Moon joyning their tinctures as  Geber    saith. With the second (which is a vegetable humidity reviving what was dead) both   principles materialls must loosed bee; & formalls, With the third permanent  incombustible & unctuous humidity  Hermes  tree is burnt to ashes. This is our fire naturall,   our ☿ , 🜍 , tincture, soule stone, born up ith wind, the vapor of metalls potentiall   [i.e. powerfull. see Medulla pag. 169 & 170.] It is invisible (in the beginning & in sun & moon or rather tis hid in the vegetable menstrum) yet   with the second water by seperation of elements its made to appeare like a   glorious cleer water [videlicet in the hower of conjunction , or by distilling ]   Of our menstrue by labor exuberate & with it may bee made 🜍  of nature   [i.e. out of the first menstrue] if it bee well acuate & circulate into a pure spirit [by   sublimations]. Then dissolve thy base with it diversly. And when thou hast made   true calcination till thy base by oft subtiliating lightly flow as wax on metall   [being an unctuous rotten matter begining to liquefy after the end of putrefaction]   then loos it with thy vegetable menstruall [i.e. the second which now begins to appear   & circulate] till you have oyle thereof in colour bright then is your menstrue visible   to sight, [i.e. the third which as hee said before is thus made to appeare by the second. But   note that  Eyreneus  makes all these 3 to bee the third.] And oyle is drawn out in   colour of gold or like thereto, out of our fine red lead, which  Raimund  said when hee   was old would comfort him for when he was nigh dead hee made thereof aurum potabile   to revive him [this oyle is ☉  potabile tis an unctuous red humidity   drawn out of laton or the Elixir of a middle temper twixt the body & washing spirit & it may bee still exalted for in the next   words he saith] this oyle & vegetable menstruall may bee be so together circulate either so by labour exuberat &   made our stone our Basilisk our Cocatrise our Elixir which tinges all metalls to sun &   moon. Aurum potabile is thus made not of common Gold but our Tincture drawn out of   our base with our vegetable menstrue circulate. But calcination must bee made ere thy   Gold dissolved bee. [the base therefore is the fixed body below red lead. the base oyle is a red fiery   moysture drawn out of it the life of & the vegetable menstrue a white washing spirit, these are   the 3 dimensions of a body ever manifested by solution which must bee circulate  together into the Elixirs & out of them be made ☉  potabile by a new dissolution &  calcination, which derives its cheif vertue from the base oyle. Gate 1. Calcination.    Wee make calces of 3 degrees white & red ere our base be perfit, they must bee fluxible as wax   (i.e. 2 🜍 s  & Elixir) they are not made in lesse then a yeare. As for the proportion   Let the body be sotelly filed with ☿  as much so subtiled, One of the ☉  2 of the ☽  till   all together like pap be don. Then make ☿  4 to the sun, 2 to the ☽  etc   Thus is   there 3 of the spirit & of the body 3, & for the unity of the substance spirituall one more   then of the substance corporall [ie ☉  + ☽ =3 & ☿ =3+1.] But  Bacon  took 3   of the spirit to 1 of the body. If the water be equall to the earth the solution will bee   surer & better. Observe potters loom & make it not too moyst. <6.3r> Page Image5    Turn earth to water that to air by levigation & that to fire. Then goe backward   & into thy water turn thy fire & air into earth. Thus in 3 circulations through black white & red thy base will   bee consummate Second Gate Solution.    Bodys are attenuated & eclipsed in our first menstrue. Of the composition the ☉  is the father   the ☽  the mother & ☿  the mediator & these are but 2 agent & patient, 🜍  & ☿ . & out   of these is ingendred a meane mercury unctuus burning the bodys more fiercely then fire   & dissolving them to water, which we call night in the north. Let thy glas bee well closed   & never hotter then that thou mayst feele & hold with thy bare hand. [note that solution is   not perfect till after putrefaction. Third Gate seperation    Seperation parts the Elementall qualitys till they bee turnd into the fifth degree. Earth turns   to water black & blew, water into air under very white, & air into fire. But of   seperation we must say more. Draw oyle incombustible & water out of the stone (as  David  said)   & that with an easy fire first moist then dry, drawing out flegm patiently & then the other  natures, dry up the earth till it be thirsty by calcining, & then make it drink up its moisture   again [videlicet which hung in the concavity of the glas whereby is made a second liquefaction.] Thus   seperation thou must often make dividing the matter into 2 parts one grosse earth below, blew & fixt   the other spirituall & flying, but they must bee reduced into one thing. [i.e. black & dry calx]   Then [putrefaction ended] Oyle & water [i.e. base oyle & the base it swims on with water   [vegetable menstrue] shall destill & through her help recieve moving [bee washed dissolved   attenuad & vivified till all sublime together into a glorious ☿ . All this might seem to be spoked of the   salt but that hee immediatly saith] beware that nothing exhale, but close thy glase with a stopple   & then melt the top together [Note that seperation & dissolution begin together   when  the bodys begin to hold of each other   Distill the ☿  [first menstrue] oft till it bee quit from all feces bright heavy & then   some multiply it with   & such like substances which wee reject. [Thus he continues   to describe our ☿  calling it the poyson out of which our Treacle is made.  Hermes  calls it   his wind flying up from ☉  & ☽  & elevating part of the bodys with it & drawing out their fatnes   whence he bid,] rais up the birds from their nest & bring them again to rest [that is dissolv   & congeale] Turn essentiall ☿  into wind for water with water will ascend, then make them   descend. To conclude let thy water be 7 times sublimed or there will bee noe dissolution 
  putrefaction nor colours for want of fire   There are 4 fires 1 agent nature this is our dragon burning more feircely then that of hell   2 Of nature the third menstruall, & it is in all things. 3 Innaturall which is occasionale 4  Externall Fourth Gate Conjunction.    Conjunction is the copulation of dissevered qualitys its 2 fold, gross when the body is   reincrudate with ☿  & propper after seperation . It is also 3 fold 1 diptive or   [amalgamation] 2 Triptive of body soul & spirit the body retains the soule & the soul the spirit [&   this is before putrefaction & is but a confusion of the natures 3 Tetraptive of the Elements [at the   hour of conjunction.   Make the man to his wife 1 to 3 & make but 1 gros conjunction & after 5 months   increase the heat a little till the stone be white then mayst thou take it out & feed it   with bread & milk. Fifth Gate Putrefaction    Without it noe true seperation of Elements nor conjunction can bee made. Its  defined a killing of the bodys in order to regeneration, a division of things three   [🜄  🜃  🜁 ] <6.3v> Page Image6    Let them ly six weeks together without motion in the bed of conception in a  gentle heat, like the blackest black which will bee preceded by colours of the rainbow   & then by putrefying & bubling like molten pitch. After black follows white. But   it was 150 days ere the waters of  Noah s flood were dryed up. After that he plantd   his vineyard which flourishd & brought forth grapes, then bee secure for when 30 days   are gone thou shalt have grapes ruby red which is our Adrop our Vlyfer 636v6  our red lead.   Observ how timber putrefys best in variation of wet & dry so let thy matter somtime   ascend then descend, liquefy & harden etc. Keepe thy glas from suddein cold & violent   heat & make the heat so temperate that the matter vitrify not by the sides. The sixth Gate Congelation.    Wee congeale into a calx unctuous & fluxible like wax but not relenting to watr   in the air, And as blood is digested first to white then red, so is our stone   Our infant is born in the Air [in the volatile humidity in ♃ s  regimen.] Thy earth must  therefore be revived with water a soule arising from it like aire with pure water which   meane air is called our oyle or water. It is our fire our oyntment our spirit our stone   the life of all things. It goeth not out or in alone but the water (or spirit) accompanys it   & givs it motion. [see the third Gate]. These humiditys are assimilated to the menstruus   humors of a woman which issueth forth before the birth, & of which the seed is nourished   passing from thing to thing, from state to state in the glass as an infant in the womb till   the infant bee perfect. And this menstruall is drawn out of the earth by waters its colour is green at the first showing   & for that time the Sun hideth his light taking his cours through north by night, It is the   blood of Green Lyon (as  Venus  can at the begining tell) which for lack of heat had not   perfect digestion. But when tis congealed to white & fixed in its owne body, tis blood   twice digested, our milk white diadem [i.e. white 🜍 ]   Our fiery water thus accuate is our menstrue wherein our earth is losid & calcind   that they never part [tis losid by it after putrefaction & both congel together unles   there want heat for then part of this fiery water menstrue is seperated] Then   Congeale thou more water into the 3 parts of this accuate water & 1/4 th  of congeald   earth [this seemes to bee the proportion after the first conjunction of 2 to 7]   add therefore 1/4 th  part anew & congeale it as before which will shine like a slipt sword Give it therefore 1/4 th  of new   water 3 severall times & 1/2 3 other times & lastly 5/4 ths . Then putrify againe &   pass to white & red. & thou hast brought thy base to an end. This is thy matter   divided into 2 parts One to purify the body, the other to imbibe it [Hee glances at   the first sublimation & then passeth to the imbibing the white 🜍    In scala dicitur Cum Sol vadit ad  Aquilonarem  partem aqua quae egreditur   de muliere bene tunc dicitur menstruum quia cum illâ et in illa sanguis menstrualis   nondum bene digestus a corpore egreditur quae est vita eius post mortem, quae   etiam egredi incipit ab ipso [sole] in prima solutione, cuius color est viridis   Et sic menstruum vocatur aqua cum qua tribus primis mensibus administrabitur   compositum. Cum vero faetus incipit vivificari, attrahit ad se paulativè in  nutrimentum donec nascatur. Est enim menstruum sanguis non benè digestus, et bene  digestus convertitur in lac quod corona vocatur. Estque tunc sanguis bis coctus. Et paulò   post vocat hanc aquam menstruosam quae in partu  exoritur , ignem acuatam.   Dein docet natum imbibere. <6.4r> Page Image7  The Seventh    Cibation is a feeding our matter dry with milk & meat moderately till it bee   brought to the third order [that is a graduall fermenting with ☉  its calx & ☿  amalgama till   it run 1 on 1000. See  Aeyreneus  of Imbibition Cap Consule etiam Epist ad   Regem Edvardum et Exercit 15 in Turbam ubi proportiones  traducuntur . See also the Marrow of   Alchimy cap 2 stanz 30. Give it meat & drink by little & little that thou neither glut nor drown it for if it drink   too much it must have a vomit. Keep it from cold, & thus turne the wheele thrice till it   be strong & fluxible like wax.   In Scala 7 dicitur. Commisce aurum cum auro, id est, aquam cum cinere. Item seminate   aurum in terram foliatam. Item Cibatur terra margaritarum ac foliorum cum   duabus aquis mediis conclusivè. Item Opus album est opus hiemis et rubeum aestatis, nam   opus rubeum majori ignitione indiget in triplo [ie. calore externo in tripla rotatione] et  cibatione acetosâ [majori] in septuplo [forsan imbibendi numero] Item Corpus est vinculum animae   et anima spiritus; spiritus penetrat, corpus figit, anima ligat et copulat: in quibus   est nigredo est et mors frequenti irrigatione auferenda, sed quantum in humido solveris   in assando tantum desiccabis.   The Marrow of Alchymy part 1 lib 2. stanz 31 To increase the white medicine in pondus imbibe   it with warm milk before it cool, yet not too plentifully, nor forget to put meat to it.   But if it hath cooled then ferment it warily in due proportion. The red must bee   mixed with water as in the first work & in the same heat. But that quantity of milk would   drown the white, it will not recongeale more then a fourth part of water, then must another   fourth part follow & so on till it bee of courage sturdy bold then must all be close sealed & the   heate augmented, & the blacknes will continue 40 days. Stanz 42 Hee wrought common ☉    with sophick ☿  to the White Elixir. The Author of the Epistle saith he stumbled twice   in passing from the white to the red erring in imbibition cibation & fermentation.   In the hunting of the green Lyon, hee bidds make ready the ferments of ☉  & ☽  agent the kings birth   & joyne one fourth part of it with the magnet warme & put them warm into your glass, then circulate   & the best multiplication is continuall fermentation.   Exercit 12 in Turbam Natum regem non priva aere nè suffocetur.   rex vitam accipit fermento, nec citius vas aperiendum   est quam cum fermentare velis. Et tunc quamvis pro nutritione sedulo lac dandum est   tamen ita moderato rege igne ut lac non evolet, donec cum septies vel octies nutritus fuerit   robustus fiat ad tolerandum robustissimum ignem et universalem solutionem. Nam solvi   debet post plenariam nutritionem et iterum siccari in pulverem, dein iterum nutriri donec   tingat. Et solvitur aceto suo acerrimo.   Exercit 15 Postquam rex ortus est, id est anima per fermentum mediante aqua lapidi   mortuo infusa est, nutriri debet primùm modico dein majori cibo duplo scilicet ita ut Elixir   non superet cibum nisi in una parte, quia tertia partes elixiri debet excedere cibum ut per   eam concoqui possit. Tunc augmentum sic adhibitum, per omnes decoctiones species in igne   temperato continuatur. Videtur etiam dicere quod cibus est in nutrice.   See the eleventh Gate of multiplication by cibation = fermentation. The Eighth Gate Sublimation    Our Sublimation is not to the top of the vessell. But the matter must bee kept 40 days in   blacknesse. Then will the spirit & soule arise & as they putrefy sublime more & more till all   bee borne up in water, for in the air our Child must bee again borne of the water, & then   when all is white, reiterate the heaven upon the earth till all bee reincorporate which   will bee in 7 sublimations [i.e. congealings & relentings of the matter before perfect   congelation.   Wee sublime to spiritualize the body, incorporate & fix the spirit, & to clense it from filth   & sulphureous fatness. And when all is depurated the spirits will sublime up whiter then snow   & at last bee thrown down again.   Scala phil: Octava. Sublimatio et exaltatio non differunt nisi quod per sublimationem   intelligitur perfecta conjunctio fermenti cum lapide. Tunc est medicina simplex sed   non perfectissima donec per exaltationem nobilitetur. Sublimation seemes therefore   to relate to imbibition, subtiliating what the other made grosse. <6.4v> Page Image8  The Ninth Gate. Fermentation.    Some dissolv ☉  & ☽  into a lympid liquor (per acetum) & then congeale them on their medicines   other dissolve ☉  & ☽  in ☿  till they rise with the spirits subliming them up twice or thrice & then ferment   others  come  nearer & amalgam ☉  & ☽  like pap. then throw their medicines thereon. And   they erre onely in that they putrefy not nor alter beforehand. Doe therefore with ☉  & ☽  as thou   didst with the imperfect body. To the compound make ferment the fourth part: Fix water & earth   together & when the medicine as wax doth flow Then upon Malgams look thow it   throw, & when all is mixed close the glas. There is a third Ferment most secret which is the   first earth to his own water green And therefore when the Lyon doth thirst, make   him drink till his belly burst. Fermentation is incorporating the soul with the   body [i.e. 🜍  with altered ☉  ] that the body may fix the spirit [🜍  & the spirit subtiliz the body.  Therefore Gold ferment with Gold, with its owne water (that is 🜍  with earth altered) the spirits of life   going between, for the earth shall draw down his soul born up with the wind. With wind   therefore lead the soul out & in, mixing gold with gold, elements with Elements For earth   is ferment to water & water to earth. Earth is gold & soe is the soule. And what is said   of Gold understand of Silver [All this is a description of the union of 🜍  & ☉  after blacknes]   There is a way belonging to another working most excellent. Wee make a most  redolent water [vegetable menstrue] wherewith wee bring all bodys to [base] oyle which with our medicine   wee make flowing. This water wee call a quintessense which cures all disseases in Man   But this must bee don with thy base prepared as I told thee, which base is our calx. For when our   bodys bee so calcined that water soon dissolvs them to oyle, & such oyle of Sun & Moon is   ferment most fragrant to smell   In concordantia  Raimundi  et  Guidonis ,  Ripleus  ait Aurum et argent vulgare debent   cum mercurio lapidis alterari et elevari in 🜍 r  christallinum et figi et 🜍  auri debet partim   rubficari et partim ab oleo argenti et 🜍 is  in albedine detineri, et ambo 🜍 a  solis   et ☽ æ  in oleum perduci, cum quibus duobus oleis sulphura corporum (quae se habent   tanquam mineralia media inter ☿ um et fermentum) debent fermentari ut Elixira   fiant facile fusibilia. Quare decipiuntur fermentantes cum aquis aut oleis  extractis a corporibus nullo modo alteratis   Scala Nona dicit Sit fermentum 1/4 aeris. Item cinis est fermentum auri [🜍 is ] & aurum   est aqua eorum & aqua est fermentum corporis, et fermentum aquae cinis est etc: misceo ergo   Solum cum auro id est aquam cum cinere. Conjunge spiritum cum terra dealbata et calce quod   est fermentum suum & confice totum cum aqua quousque fermentetur. See Sublimation before The Tenth Gate. Exaltation    Tis almost the same with sublimation [being the Regimen in multiplication as sublimation is   in imbibition .] Tis done two ways by death & putrefaction, & by resurrection ascending up to heaven.   Hee explains this conversion of Elements aqua ex aere ex igne ex terra [See Scalam also]   Then hee adds this fyer into water, air into earth, water into fire, & earth into air.   Scala. Si praecox rubedo ante albedinem appareat, errasti, aufer itaque et cum nova   alba aqua imbibe inhuma et iterum putrefac. Primae autem albedinis signum est parvus circulus   capillaris circa latera vasis subcitrini ubi priùs post summam nigredinem apparuit  circulus albus. Cum fixatur, exalta perpetim addendo novum humorem et fige toties The Eleventh Gate Multiplication    Tis done either by solving dry pouders, or by iterate fermentation (as in that & the chapter   of cybation was tought) [i.e imbibing it warm], or by both together [i.e. projecting it on   gold & solving see Aeyreneus] The first augments its vertue the next it weight & the last both   in greater degre, for by it 1 multiplys a 1000 if thou project wisely, that is gradually.   Scala. Multiplicatur citius & in maius per fermentationem, sed in meliùs per solutionem. The Twelfth Gate. Projection    Project first on ferment [☉ ] & purg other bodys before thou projectest on them, els there will be loss. The Conclusion    Hide the altitude of thy body & show out its profundity begining its first side of latitude in the west   where the red man & his white wife destroy their first &  manifest  their second more glorious qualitys be made one. Earth & water equally proportioned is best; And   one of the earth is good & of the spirits three, which twelve to foure also of the earth may bee. Three of   the wife & one of the man then must thou take, & the lesse bee the spirits the surer will bee   calcination. Then forth into the north etc Then hast thou turned the wheel about, But yet   again turn it twice more. Then art thou master to all our secrets. After this hee reccons   up severall colours appearing before white. Then saith turn thy  bases  to oyle by water ardent, & those oyles will   by projection turn all bodys to ☉  & ☽ .  Lib 3. 182 Carmen   Niveo candore etiam fucata renidet

Aqua nostra cum   aliis rebus sui generis [coquendo scilicet] mutatur in essentiam perfectam primo mutatur   in oleum (et habes spiritum perfectum) deinde modico calore in viscositatem vel   picem duram (& habes spiritum & animam, & pix ista calore Solis perfecto   fit lapis Philosophorum & Elixir (& habes spiritum animam & Corpus) Aqua nostra   dicitur materia prima & sperma metallorum (unde creverunt omnia) aqua   vitae metallica (dans vitam & sanitatem metallis ) & Lac Virginis (Virgo   enim est antequam viro ☉ i  nubatur) & soli conjuncta tum dicitur   Mercurius & aqua vitae Philosophorum 8121r_2  sanguis veri rubri caro ossa & corpus ejus, Lac femineum   vel potius humanum quo infans nutritur. Lapis quamdiu infans cum   sanguine rubri vel viridis Draconis nunquam perfectè operabitur.   Aqua prima naturaliter facta est, & post conjunctionem parit aquam   vitae & Lac Philosophicum. Haec de materia primâ.   Has materias tres (quae sunt unum) in unum vitrum pone, putrefac, abstrahe aquam   per alembicum primo in Balneo Mariae mox super cineres 12 horis igne   lento, materiam siccam tere, aquam affunde, sine in Balneo Mariae tres dies   distilla ut priùs materia erit nigridior; repete ter semper terendo   quarta vice ne amplius tere, & inter singulas distillationes ignem   serva lentum ut mediocriter siccetur, aquam refunde dissolve sub   Alembico caeco & in singulis distillationibus 6 aut 7 guttas Phlegmatis   separa; hoc septies itera donec pondus quod antea habuit contineat   album erit & quo albius eo plus aquae suae bibit. Haec aqua  homogenea parit album & rubrum lapidem , cumque ad perfectam albedinem   perductus est, unius operis finis est initium alterius i.e. Lapidis rubri  magnesiae nostrae rubrae, aeris virginalis, auri nostri & Sulphuris nostri vivi etc. Noster mercurius sperma   aqua vitae etc extrahitur ex terra  Aethiopiae , magnesiâ etc.   Ms. Var. 259 8

Verum sine mendacio, certum & verissimum.   Quod est inferius est sicut id quod est superius: & quod est superius   est sicut id quod est inferius, ad perpetranda miracula rei unius.   Et sicut omnes res fuerunt ab uno, mediatione unius: sic omnes res   natae fuerunt ab hâc unâ re, adaptione.   Pater ejus est  Sol : Mater ejus est  Luna  : portavit illud ventus in   ventre suo : nutrix ejus est terra. Pater omnis telesmi totius mundi   est hic. Virtus ejus integra est si versa fuerit in terram.   Separabit Terram ab igne, subtile a spisso, suaviter, magno cum   ingenio. Ascendit terra in caelum , iterumque descendit in terram, &   recipit vim superiorum & inferiorum.   Sic habes gloriam totius mundi : ideo fugiet a te omnis obscuritas.   Haec est totius fortitudinis, fortitudo fortis, quia vincet omnem rem   subtilem, omnemque solidam penetrabit.   Sic mundus creatus est.   Hinc erunt adaptiones mirabiles, quarum modus est hic.   Itaque vocatus sum  Hermes Trismegistus , habens tres partes Philosophiae   totius mundi.   Completum est quod dixi de operatione Solis. Ex libro de Metallorum metamorphosi. Caput 1    pag 5. Ex plumbi minerâ per conveniens temporis spatium abque molestiâ dimissa, ☽  reperitur copiosa   p.6.  Mercurius  potest cum omnibus metallis succesive coqui ut eorum  crasim  & proprietates   successive  <illeg.> 8212r_1  sub eodem colore & fluxus formâ. Item ☿ us   i  fiat  martialis  per   essentiae tantummodò  Martialis  in eodem per artificium sagax decoctionem & iste ♂ alis fiet ♀ eus  per veneris fermentum deinde  Lunaris   Jovialis   Solaris , multâ   visibili mutatione factâ nisi quod unus ☿  sit altero purior. Sic Mirabilis Antimonii   mercurius etiam varietur. Et si hi mercurii sic mutati cum mercurio vulgari   misceantur & decoquentur, istum permutabunt ut in suum naturam ut easdem habebit   proprietates cum mercurio sibi mixto. Et haec ego manibus expertus sum. Sed haec   expertu difficilia & a Philosophis occulta tanquam clavis artis a nobis hic patefactus.. Nec perinde est quodnam primò metallum   aut minerale agrediatur, quave viâ pergat sed hic requiritur sagacitas ingeniosa & insuper praxis  alias volens nolens errabit   pag 9. Vulgaritur notum est mutare ♂  in ♀  per acidum Vitrioli Stalagma, ♀  in  ,   in ♃ , ♃  in ☽  etc. Sed soli veri Philosophi norunt substantiam dari mediam in mineram &   metallum occultam, cujus una extremitas est minera altera Metallum, quarum commistae   virtutes caelestes unum aliquod efficiunt metallum anonymum imò non Metallum sed Chaos   unde omnia 7 Metalla possum educere totum Volatilis spiritus. Et Chalybs a Novi Luminis authore vocatum   & est operis principium & clavis   pag 12. In omni motus dantur termini & in omni generatione terminus a quo & ad quem sunt iidem. Elixar corpora imperfecta penetrans, quod sibi simile  invenerit, apprehendit & praepollenti suâ vi ab ignis flagrantis violentia defendit & fixitate sua  praepollenti retinet quod alias cum praedominante impuritate avolaret interea partes   impurae ab igne consumuntur, & quod purum est manet solum.   Caput 4 pag 33.   non in   at in ☉  solo est multiplicabilis. In Elixare   albo ☿  praedominatur & digestio crudior sicut in generatione faeminae quae ab eodem   semine cum mari fit.   Caput 5 pag 36. semen mettalli per totum corpus diffunditur, secus quam fit in   vegetalibus & animalibus & hinc respectu Lapidis ☉  totum est materia; cum profunditas   manifestatur totum est sperma, quod circulando recipit vim superiorum &  inferiorum & tum fit tota forma seu caelestis virtus aliis hanc virtutem  communicandi capax. Semen aquae immediatè residet & per totam diffunditur licet   aquae sit minima pars unde totam aquam vocamus semen, sicut totum granum   vocamus semen. Nec ab aquâ potest separari nisi ratione sola. Et hoc semen   ab antiquis vocatur fermentum occultum, venenum, ignis invisibilis, secretò agens.   pag 37 Veteres aliquando dicunt ignem esse aquam aliquando in aqua, quare   hanc distinguunt ini animam & spiritum, quorum prior est medium posterior virtus   activa per istud medium. Nutritio vegetatio & multiplicatio rerum semper fit   in humido (succo chylo vel ☿ o ) & per humidum.   Caput 7. pag 42. Interus nucleus, sal christallinum, succus  Lilii  albi cum suo 🜍 e  occulto rubro   (quae Hermeticorum ☉  & ☽  sunt) ex impuro Chao producitur quod est difficillimum in arte   et cum multa tarditate assequendum . Qui hanc clavem seu scaturiginem habet   is non tantum operis universalis sed omnium particularium dominus est. Ait etiam subjectum nostrum esse Metallicum sed non metallum nec minera sed   metalla et minerae et minerae et Metalla, quia omnium illorum natura est in unâ re   quae vocatur Electrum minerale, magnesia, lunaria. Item Dicit se omni horâ ex   nostro subjecto corpus metallicum praeparare posse. Et quod haec radix mineralis seu   astri fluens septem radiis constans flos debet ab inquina mentis purgari et scaturiginem   universalem exprimi qui valde tener et volatilis est .   pag 533. Cum venenosi vapores lapidem reliquissent, ecce flos albus paulatim   apparuit quem primò conspexi, postea flos ruber per singulare misterium seperatim in parte   inferiori aparebat. Sed de eo hic non ago, posthac fortasse, cùm de mirabililbus quae   in horâ conjunctionis & nativitatis apparere solent dicturus sum, de hâc etiam   re agetur. Ad ☉  potabile inevitabiliter necessarium est ut Elementorum portae unanimiter   reserantur . Et in hac solutione universali hoc agendum ut per convenientem   ignis gradum solvens & solutum in spiritualitate et corporalitate unum fiant   Item cùm philosophi aiunt ☉  per corrosivum solvi, hoc non  intelligendum est de ☉  vulgari. Nam aurum Vulgi in materiam primam reduci   non potest et regenerari. Per solam verò tincturam ad album praeparatam   aurum omnem metallicam suam substantiam adeò perdit ut nunquam post   ad metallicam naturam reduci potest.   pag 387 Nullum corrosivum aut aqua metallica species dissolvit praeter   eam quae cum illis sub eadem forma permanet.   pag 388 Hoc corrosivum seu aqua solvens extrahitur ex materia quae   juxta auri scaturiginem reperitur [i.e in Aurifodinis ]. Item videte ut virga mineralis   naturae naturaliter & artificialiter indicio sit: hac enim ratione metallici   et elementalis auri minerarum vestigia & indicia qua vos non fallent deprehendetis.   Astrale aurum quod attinet cujus supra meminimus illud supra nos est   quod Artistae operationi non convenit.   Item Artis chemicae honos et veritas non solum scriptis confirmatur, sed potiùs   per experientiam ex 4 Elementorum aerario desumptam, in quo quintum   esse et Generalissimi Archeus Generalis imperium habet, stabilitur.   pag 389. Scribit de corrosivo in dulcedinem vertendo per  rectificationem. Dein ait, Aurum potabile extra processum praeparationis 
  materiae seu solventis universalis impossibile, et hujus corrosivi usum et   applicationem, esse artem ad tincturae universalis compositionem summè   necessariam. Qua ratione autem hoc corrosivum in summam dulcedinem sine   omni solventis a soluto separatione convertatur, ut aurum fluxile & potabile   fiat, sic instruo. Arcem fortissimam 4 Elementorum essentiam cum   Gubernatore inhabitante oppugnate et Gubernatorem captivum ducite quod vix   aut ne vix quidem in quarta aut quintâ oppugnatione succedet. Estote fidei   clypeo ense spiritus et salutis galea instructi, et ad sextam, septimam,  decimamve oppugnationem accingete et intrepide Et animode diu pugnate donec   dux septem planetarum illorumque essentia, per ascensum et descensum scalarum   atque frequentem exitum et introitum fatigentur ac tandem manus dare  cogantur. Atque ita non tantùm multorum astrorum arcem cum suo duce  incorporato accipietis, verum et pulcherrimam totius provinciae civitatem   in diaphaneitatis splendore & divitiis capietis. Haec sunt spolia quae ab  elementis regeneratis mediante ista rectificatione Et purgatione accipiuntur.   pag 390. Si Nucleum et Philosophiae centrum cum auro potabile ejusque processu   hic non discitis, actum erit de vobis: nam in hoc tractatulo auri tam metallici quam   Elementalis generatio & ortus ostenditur.  Geber  ait ☉  potabile non fieri nisi per naturam   igneam ipsius quintae essentiae. Addit: Principia et proprietates istae quamvis  perfectiùs ex primo ente quam suo metallo desumuntur, tamen multam operationem  manualem requirunt ne virtus illorum principiorum generativa per incongruum ignis <9.3r> Page Image5  gradum interrumpatur. Per multos annos Artista plerumque sistitur propter   summam tam artificiosè graduati ignis in inveniendo difficultatem.   pag 392. Quicunque Chaos illud Metaphysicum ex tenebrosa sua potentia ad actum   potest deducer se faelicem praedicet. Item ut quatuor elementa operam auri  potabilis porrigant necesse est. Duas operationes in se continet. Prima est specierum   ex quibus paratur praeparatio. Altera est conservatio illarum per salem astralem   quod pallum seu vestem honoris custodit & cum amoris vinculo unit ut fiat   liquidum et potabile aliisque usibus inserviens.   pag: 392. Quadrangulum Universalis in rotunditatem & punctum circuli adducite   [In hac appendice observo 1 Quod ad ☉  potabile requiritur aqua solvens sive corrosiva   et ☉  Elementale. p 38 4  2 Quod hoc corrosivum cum auro Elementali in dulcedinem sine solventis et soluti separatione vertendum 
  est rectificando (alias non solvet aurum Metallicum) idque septies vel decies. 3 Quod   hac operatione feces purgantur & paratur universalissima tinctura ad album cum qua ☉  potest   solvi (idque ut ait  Sendivogius , sicut glacies in aqua calida). 4 Quod praeparatio   per astrale ☉  est nimium debilis. Et quod non aliâ viâ quàm universalissimâ   fit potabile. 5 Quod de ignis gradu summè cavendum ne excedat modum. 6   Quod videtur innuere tincturam ex solo ☉  elementali esse medicinalem et ☉  potabile   sed an auri vulgaris solutionem includit dubito, cum illud sine labore tunc  dissolvitur. 7: Quod videtur non de praeparatione salis solitariâ loqui sed potiùs de   sublimatione aquae mercurialis cum suo 🜍 e . Nam dicit arcem 4 Elementorum ( alias   arcem astrorum, aerarium elementorum etc] cum duce incluso expugnandum esse.   Item quod tinctura ad album solvit aurum. Item quod species debent   conservari per salem astralem. Item quod ☿  et 🜍  embrionatum purganda et   praeparanda sunt. Tamen de hoc dubitatur siquidem caput 5 est De corrosivo in   dulcedinem vertendo per rectificationem. 8 Quod vocat reserationem  portarum Elementorum, solutionem universalem .  [Credo hic nihil adeptus  <9.3v> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <9.4r> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <9.4v> Page Image6  Lucerna Salis Philosophorum.    p 25 Sal est triplex 1 centralis   [ceu seminalis] qui in centro elementorum , qualificatione astrorum, per spiritum mundi perpetim   generatur, et radiis solis et ☽ ae  in mari Philosophico gubernatur. 2 spermaticus ceu   domicillium seminis invisibilis qui in suavi naturali calore per putrefactionem   ex se dat formam et vegetabilitatem suam, quatenus illud semen invisibile   volatilissimum non externo aestu, vel alio contrario et violento accidenti   dissipatur ac destruatur, sic enim nihil amplius exinde generari potest   Tertius sal est ultima materia omnium rerum post destructionem earundem   in illis inventa et superstes reperta: estque initium operis philosophici.   Ad hunc obtinendum oportet metallorum generationem et prima astra   novisse quae omnia ex una radice profluunt. In Hyle Philosophorum est   fons salinus.   p 27 Unicum est artis subjectum in quo tota consistit, est lapis et non lapis   vocatur lapis similitudinarie quia minera ejus ex antris terrae desumpta   teritur, et post ejus destructionem iterum fit lapis incremabilis & caerifluus.   Est ens auri, et semen ejus necessario debes adhibere   p. 27 describens Elixir sive ♁  maturum et fixum ait esse caerifluum   in omni liquore solubile colorando per totum, metalla penetrans post semihorae   fluxum in ☉  perficiens, id quod strepitu indicatur.   p 32 In molli monte subjecti quod natura in metallicam formam adduxit   sed imperfectum reliquit faciliùs foveam fodere possis et aquam ponticam   haurire quae sola et nusquam alia unda apta nata est cum suâ propria   farina & solari fermento in pastam formari, etc: esque in recenti receptaculo  9414r_1 
  saturni cujus aliquotuplex est proles: viz stibium triumphans etc.   p 34 Solutio est triplex 1 corporis crudi 2 terrae Philosophicae 3 in  multiplicatione. Sed ut aliquid facilius solvatur, prius debet calcinari et a faecibus   purgari et aquis opitulantibus  siquis  adhibeantur, ut nihil heterogenei  remaneat; sed maximè cavendum a nimio calore ne virtus generativa destruatur   Ex magnesia primò sperma colligimus, collectum  putrefacimus, solvimus, dividimus, purificamus unimus & sic totum   opus leniter coquendo perficitur. Per seipsum & in seipso lento calore putrescit &   in humorem primordialem resolvitur, incalescens quoque per se in causa   est quod lapis sudat lachrimas. Et si rectè procedis apparabit atra  nebula deorsum et spiritus albus supra.   p 38 Cum elementa in fimo digessisti (nam absque putrefactione spiritus (sive ☿  in specie 🜄 ae   clave  a   corpore separari nequit) tunc divide lapidem in 2 partes, 4 Elementa quorum volatile   est aer et aqua fixum vero terra et ignis (inter quae tamen aqua ac terra   duntaxat cernuntur). Volatile est vita fixi et fixum fermentum volatilis.   utrumque ex eadem radice sunt Et in formâ aqua conjungi debent. Terram Ergo calcinâ   in humido tepente fimo equino donec albescat et pinguis videatur. Istud est 🜍  non urens et per majorem digestionem potest fieri rubeum sed album esse   oportet priusquam rubeat, et si album adest in vase tunc non debet extrahi   sed tantum coqui donec rubescat. <9.5r> Page Image7  Out of Lucerna Salis Philosophorum.    pag 52. De Lapidis efficaciâ. Primò Deus trinus et unus ipsiusque opera   creationis redemptionis et regenerationis, nec non futurae beatitatis gloriosae   status ex ipso elucent. Secundò sanat omnes morbos ad vitae terminum   ubi spiritus, ceu extincta lucerna placide emigrat & pertransit in manus   Dei. Tertio transmutat metalla, transtingit christallum et ignobiles lapides in   nobilissimas gemmas. At cùm metalla in aurum transformanda sunt, oportet   priùs cum auro fermentari alias enim nimiam ejus et supremam subtilitatem   imperfecta metalla ferre nequeunt, sed in projectione damnum foret. Si ista   medicina cum aquâ sui albi vel rubri mercurii ex qua ipsa praeparata est   iterum dissolvitur et coagulatur tunc ejus tingendi vis singulis vicibus decies   major evadet.  pag 60 Catalogus Auctorum lectu utilissimorum

Radix artis est sapo sapientum et est minera   omnium salium & dicitur sal amarum quia oritur de minera maris est acutior omnibus salibus et cum eo calcinantur corpora et fiant resolutiones et coagulationes Elixir. et nota primò   quod nullum argentum potest fieri nisi omnia prius solvantur secundò quòd omnis solutio   debet fieri in proprio sanguine, vel appropriato i.e. in aqua ☿ ii  quae dicitur aqua   Draconis. Tertiò quod illa aqua Draconis debet fieri per Alembicum sine omni   aliâ re additâ et quod in faciendo ipsum est maximus faetor. Quartò quòd   cùm illâ aquâ potest solvi amalgama, corpus et spiritus, cinnabrium, et breviter   omnia & singula quae sunt de naturâ ipsius. Quinto quod illa aqua debet esse   munda ergo non debet fieri nisi de dracone purgato: purgatur autem ipsum ter   elevando, et inde ipsum vivificando.   pag 119, sumatur ponde grani 1 post exitum Regis et in albo vino dissolvatur in   argenteo vase, tingetur vinum citrino colore, quod aegris administretur paulo post   mediam noctem: uno dic sanabitur si morbus sit unius mensis, si anni infra 12   dies, & infra mensem cuiuscunque sit durationis. Ad conservandam salutem sumendum   ex eo parum singulis cannis bis sub initium veris et autumni. Sic ait  Donis Zach.    Ibidem. Aurora consurgens et clangor Buccinae aiunt quod Antiqui sapientes 4   principales effectus in hac Thesauro repererunt. 1 Dare hominibus Salutem . 2   tingere Metalla. 3 Lapides ignobiles in gemmas pretiosas transmutare. 4 Omne   Vitrum ductile facere sive malleabile. Internis morbis medetur in vino sumptum,   vitii s oculorum doloribus capitis aut membrorum vulneribus etc ungendo medetur. cicatrices   per eam complanantur, vinum corruptum et acidum si ei permiscetur reparatur.  calculus solvitur si bibatur venenum expellit sumendo, occiduntur et vermes si in   pulvere sumatur, rugas et maculas in facie debet ungendo et spondet faciem  juvenilem mulieribus, in partu sumpta auxiliatur, faetum emortuum emplastrando educit   urinam provocat, coitum excitat et auget, ebrietatem prohibet, memoriam inducit etc   nota quod haec semper medicinae apotheticae ad morbum deputatae est permiscenda.   Ibidem.  Rosarius  inquit quod facit hominem juvenescere ut aquila & per 500   annos vivere et ampliùs ut aliqui vixerunt qui usi fuerunt eo tribus   vicibus in hebdomade quantum granum sinopis. Si pulvis teneatur ad nares   in partu laborantium liberantur, ut ait  Hermes .  Hollandus  in opere saturni ait   si homo quispiam veneni pessimi integram libram in corpore haberet et in ipsâ   horâ unam drachmam hujus medicinae in vino tepido biberet iste nihil damni   a veneno sentiret.   pag 125. Uncia una projicit super 25 marcas ☽ ae  vel ♀ is , super 18 marcas   plumbi vel stanni, super 15 marcas ☿ ii  in crucibulo calefacti vel plumbo congelati. Materia   mox spuma crassiore contegetur, operatione completa crepitus fiet ac si crucibulum   frangeretur, tandem liquefiat et erit ☉ . Verùm si dictum pondus non  obsevatur, in cineritio sine plumbo per tres horas fulminanda est, et reliquum in fundo   purum est caemento regali sex horis latius purgandum ut quod non tingitur  consumatur solumque aurum purissimum.   pag 128. Rubea medicina ex chrystallo facit praestantissimum rubinum ut et ex   aliis vilioribus gemmis. Cùm alba verò possunt Adamantes unionesque parari   vel e parvis eorum majores pro lubitu fieri confici quoque possunt smaragdi <9.6r> Page Image9   <The following five lines are indented in the manuscript in order to give space to the the supralinear insertion ending in "Elixir." at the top of 5v, which extends onto this page.> 
  saphiri, chrysoliti, Berylli, et alii ita tamen ut appropriati colores adhibeantur   nam medicina liquat demollit et aperit gemmas & inducit colorem et iterum claudit &   locat in omnes proprietates naturalium gemmarum. Reddit quoque simplex   vitrum malleabile ut Philosophi testantur et  Lullius  ejusmodi confectiones   in Theoricâ et Praxi sui lapidarii tradidit   pag 129. Si illius medicinae in aqua dissolutae quantitatem aliquam in cor   ceu centrum trunci vitis injeceris flores et folia nascentur in mense maio.  <The remainder of this page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <9.6v> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <9.7r> Page Image10    Lucerna salis p 39 Aurum sapientum est aqua clara super qua fertur   spiritus domini, spirituale redditur; ducitur per Alembicum spiritus, Terra   ejus remanet nigra quae tamen non apparebat antea, et nunc seipsum   resolvit, fitque itidem aqua spissa desiderans vitam nobilem ut sibi restituatur.   Prae  siti  enim seipsum dissolvit et frangit; Idque admodum conducit ei. Quia nisi fieret aqua et   oleum non posset etiam spiritus et anima eidem misceri ut nunc est absque omni   separabilitate.   p 40. Sal noster externe ut nix albet interne rubet, et ex eo cuncti colores   evolvi possunt, dulcissimus est et spiritus invisibilis in eo habitans penetrationis   vehementiâ aequalis est fortissimo fulgori   p 41 Metallorum intrinseca sunt ens aureum, extrinseca vero morte et execratione   circundata. Nam possident 1 duram quandam lapidosam crassitiem a minerâ tractam   2 laethalem aquam faetidam, 3 in aqua istâ nonnullam mortificatam terram, et quarto   interemptoriam furibundam venenosam qualitatem. Quibus impuritatibus separatis   apparet nobile ens aureum sal summe sublimatus christallinus instar nivis albus   et ut caera fluens. Hic sal initialiter apparentiam salis non habet tamen est sal   et sub prima incaeptione faetidus et niger, et in processu laboris aemulatur  coagulum cruoris tandemque clarescet et albescet, seipsum solvendo et coagulando   p 47 De matrimonio servi rubicundi cum faemina alba,  Sendivogius  pauca   haec verba tantùm reliquit: Unica res sola mixta aquâ Philosophicâ.  Grasseus    (ut ejus discipulus mihi narravit) primâ vice non potuit efficere quin 🜍   supernataret unde ad hoc resciscendum punctum novum suscepit iter, postea tamen   propinquissimam artis viam calcavit perficiendo in 30 diebus cum alii tamen   primò 7 & postea 10 mensibus coquunt.   p 49 Duplex est conjungendi ratio, humida et sicca, habente sole suae aquae   tres partes & uxore 9, vel 2 ad 7. Projice terram in matrem suam ex   quâ venit.   p 52 Opus creationis redemptionis regenerationis et futurae beatitudinis ex ipso   elucescunt. Sanat omnes morbos, et extincta lucernâ anima placide emigrat   Transmutat omnia metalla. tingit christallum et lapides ignobiles in gemmas  nobilissimas. Sed ad metalla tingenda debes primò fermentare cum ☽ a  et ☉ e  et ad   fermentum sufficit primâ vice fermenti granum nam postea potes augere tincturam. Metalla etiam  purganda sunt. Si dissolvas in aquâ sui albi vel rubri mercurii ex qua praeparata est ejus tingendi vis singulis   vicibus decies augebitur, idque quoties repetere lubet licebit.   Citations. p 104 Duplex est corporum solutio, in ☿ um  per ☿ um  et in aquam ☿ alem    prima requiritur ad particularia estque propriùs resolutio; secunda est ad universalia   quae non fit per solam dissolutionem 🜍 is  immaturi in ☿ um , sed per putrefactionem   corporis et spiritus in humido. Eadem in Rosario et Richardo habentur.   p 105. Solutio debet fieri in sanguine proprio vel appropriato i.e. in aqua draconis   per Alembicum (non absque faetore) factâ: cum qua potest solvi amalgama, cinnabrium   et omnia quae sunt de naturâ ejus. Priusquam conjungas aes et aquam [siccam] vide ut aqua sit aliquoties   destillata et tandem in statum suum et descensum restituta, ipsumque aes   combustum, candidum et sincerum factum  Aristhenes 9717r_1  in Turba. exercitio 5.   p 117. Duplex est pondus artis, commune in quo non est diversitas sed novem   et pars decima. De pondere imbibitionis, quot partis (nempe) ignis potest imbibere   natura melius scit quam artifex. Aliud est spirituale pondus in secundo opere, estque   diversum secundum diversos. Athonus in Turba ponit 1/3 partem ignis et secundas terrae,  Morienus  1/4 ignis et 3 terrae.  Hermes  vero docet quadrantam partem addere non simul   sed ex quarto ad quartum, id est in 4 diebus pro qualibet quartâ. Consilium conjugii.   et practica lapidarii. Nam medicina demollit et apperit gemmas et inducit   colores et postea iterum claudit. Si dissolvatur in aquâ et in cor vel centrum   trunci arboris injiciatur facit multo magis vegetabilem etc   129 Ex plumbo nostro extrahitur oleum aurei coloris quocum si lapidem post primam   fixionem ter vel quater sublimaveris, excusabit te ab omni labore  solutionum    et congelationum, propterea quod auget penetrabilitatem et effectum medicinae supra   modum, ut nihil sit in mundo secretius. Unde dico (quod veteribus foret  incredibile) si sciveris oleum hoc ab aqueitate seperare et laboraveris in modo   mixtionis jam dicto, poteris in 30 diebus lapidem componere. Solutiones quidem   et coagulationes cito fiunt, at si fieret sublimatio ejus, credo tincturam   multùm ampliari, elige utrum vis. Elixir ex amica re constat (Parmend in Turb sermo 11 Aurum nummos, numosque   aurum fieri facite pro nostro aere) est et compositus (Rosinus 48 Nullus lapis in   arte generatur nisi quem philosophi ex suis rebus composuerunt) ex duobus (corpore in   tabulas coaptato, et aquâ quae est permanens postquam regitur; humido et sicco; vili   et pretioso masculo et faemina) ex tribus, ex quatuor (Menald in Turb ser 25 aes   est ista 4 corpora. Pardaris sermo 43 sulphura sunt animae quae in 4 fuerunt   occulta corporibus) Et ex pluribus constat Ixir (Bonellus sermo 49 Aqua munda   quae ex 🜍 e  est, non ex solo 🜍 e  verum ex pluribus composita rebus quae unum 🜍 r  facta sunt ex pluribus 🜍 ibus ). Quippe ex pluribus speciebus unum quid  componitur quod dicitur Magnesia corpus magnesiae, aes magnesiae, sulphur   magnesiae plumbum aeris albar aeris, aurum nostrum, pecunia nostra etc (sermo 73   prima compositio viz corpus magnesiae ex pluribus fit rebus, quamvis unum quid factae sint, uno deinde   nomine quod priores albar aeris dixerunt.)   p 839 Ixiris sic ex pluribus compositi (i.e. 🜍 is ) radix et fundamentum est aes nostrum quod suo   regimine ad Ixir deduci oportet ( jam posui principium demonstrationis   specierum ex quibus fit Ixir;) et hujus aeris nostri regimen est ut fiat venenum   vel rubigo tingens (i.e. [ut putrefaciendo activitatem  sortiatrur  et separetur   a ☿ o  et iterum junctum decoquatur ad finitatem. Bonus sermo 37 aes nostrum aqua ablui perenni Philosophi jusserunt. Anaxag   ser 54 qui aurum in rubiginem vertit et venenum, propositum jam invenit)   Rubigo autem sive venenum tingens ex aere nostro praedicto non fit nisi tingatur   ( [id est per solutionem in aqua perenni.] Nam aes nostrum vix aliquo regimine vulgi   possit corrumpi quin in seipsum redeat. Quinetiam cum purgatum fuerit, color   ejus et tinctura sibi tantum sufficit et in mixtione cum altera diminuitur, quare debet regi donec tingat intensiori   colore quam habet dum manet in sui naturâ, hoc autem regimen debet esse   tale, per quod in pulverem, qui propter multitudinem intensioris coloris sit   fere niger ut color combusti sanguinis vel tyrii vel purpurei coloris. Et tunc   propter excessum coloris minima ejus pars admixta magnae corporis   minoris luminis tingit ad sufficientiam coloris luminis majoris cum  transmutatione et tincturâ invariabili & fixâ, propter virtutem et fixionem quam a   regimine et rebus tingentibus acquisivit. Monachus in Turb: sermo 25 Eo quod   aes non colorat nisi colorem, quia coloratum colorat.) Et hoc corpus Ixir est   ut fermentum in pasta.   p 840 Aes nostrum tingitur primà ab aquâ permanente vel argento   vivo de cambar, vel argento vivo ex masculo, vel res per mixtionem et regimen  Ethel  facta (i.e. aquâ nostrâ siccà ex nostris rebus compositâ est suo regimine perfectâ.   Parmenides sermo 2 Aurum vertitur in rubignem per aquam permanentem   Mosius sermo 44 Argentum vivum quod tingit ☉  est argentum vivum de cambar.) Aes nostrum iterum   ex ignis actione tingitur <9.8v> Page Image13    Ibidem. Itaque tingens Ixir ex aere et  aquâ permenente constat. Ad cujus aquae   confectionem duplex est compositio. Prima est ex rebus siccis i.e. ex quatuor  elementis sive corporibus quae vocantur sulphura terrae, et in aequali proportione miscenda   sunt, compositum verò dicitur aes nostrum vel corpus magnesiae etc ut supra. Secunda   est ex praedictis siccis cum humido sive ex argento vivo mixto cum corpore  magnesiae, et compositum dicitur magnesia. Spiritus in corpore coagulabitur et corpus in   spiritum solvetur, totumque fiet unum. Corporis spissitudo attenuabitur immunditia   curabitur, et subtilitas ignea in ejus ventre occulta (quae tinctura ejus et anima   est) in altero spiritu salvabitur occulta (Plato sermo 2). Unde aiunt si sciveris dare   ignem, ignis et azoth tibi sufficiunt. Est et argentum vivum ignis et venenum igneum quia   per corpus magnesiae diffunditur sicut venenum per corpus humanum, (Alleg. dist 5   Venenum igneum est aqua nummosa nondum perfecta). Continua ergo praefatum   regimen donec corpus in praedicto spiritu adeo subtiliatum fuerit et dissolutum ut sit  incorporeum sine tactu, amboque tactu levia et inpalpabilia ad instar butyri vel cerebri   tunc per ignem in Ethel exaltetur antequam nostro misceatur aeri. ( Astanus  sermo 42   Aes quod philosophi cum res paraverint ac conjuges ac se coadunantes conjunxerint,  ascendit ex eis aqua aurea). Haec autem sublimatio fit igne levi ut purificatum   exaltetur ad supremum vasis terrestreitate grossa quae tincturam et ingressum impedit   in Ethel fundo relictâ. Et hoc sublimatum est argentum vivum de cambar, aqua permanens   gume urina etc quocum aes nostrum tingitur. Estque primi operis complementum.   pag 843. Secundum opus est ut aes nostrum solum ab hac aqua in rubiginem   tingentem vertamus (Astanus ser 66 Rubigo est secundum opus, quod solo auro fit) Estque hujus   operis principium ut aqua praefata permanens dividatur in 2 partes. Cum una miscebitur   aeris portio (1 ad 3 vel 2 ad 7) ut in ea solvatur et diruatur in terram, et altera servatur ad   impotationem diruti. At pro aeris faciliori commixtione, debet primo in laminas   produci et aqua commisceri et  coqui   40  diebus donec solvatur et unum fiant (Aristeus sermo 10   Corpus demonstratum in tenues tabulas coaptate etc) . In ejus decoctione omnes   colores evolvuntur niger albus, croceus et rubeus. Primo coquitur 40  diebus  ad nigredinem   cujus solutionis signum est atra nubes, dein ista nigredo aquis salsis vel dulcibus abluitur   donec fiat ut nummus liquefactus , et cum incipit apparare albedo, iterum coquitur donec   fiat nummosus albus lapis ut marmor vel sal corruscans (Lucas sermo assate per dies 7   donec fiat ut marmor corruscans) et hunc lapidem vocamus Aiar vel boricum vel   Aticos. Et huc usque fiat ignis lenis, vase clauso ne spiritus exeat sed in eo  coaguletur, non totus sed pars ejus tertia tantum, residuo (in spiritus secundi cambar)   pro opere secundo relicto. Compositione in lapidem marmoreum sic coagulata, in   tende ignem ut diruatur donec in terram vel cinerem croceum vel subviridem   et aurei fere coloris   pag 846 . Lapide sic in terram aliquatenus coloratam verso imbuite   septem vicibus cum altero aquae dimidio coquendo qualibet vice donec in ea   siccetur aqua et colores appareant, nam cum sicca fuerit facile bibit humoris   residuum. Et harum impotationum tempus est 150 dies 21 diebus cuilibet  impotationi tributis. [Hermes tractatus 1 ait succus vitis sapientum in 150 diebus extrahitur   ejusque numerus in fine 30 peragitur] Fit autem impotatio quandoque dicti cineris   per aquam quae ab eo ascenderat in congelatione praefata [Arist sermo 10 Athelia   vel aqua quae ex eo exivit imbibite. Acsubofen 9818v_1  sermo 14 Accipite humorem   quam ejecit illud et cum eo paulatim imponite Diomedes sermo 29 Reddite   ei sudorem suum et iterum neci date] . In his autem imbibitionibus sit ignis lenis   ut anima non fugatur a cinere sed in eo remaneat . Quibus finitis <9.9r> Page Image intenso igne recoque per 40 dies ut fiat pulvis impalpibilis Tyrii coloris   Et hic est Yxir sive venenum nostrum quod nummos tingit et in aurum vertit   et hominem in perfecta salute conservat.  14

  latinitate donata per Authorem Chymicae Vannus   10a   1   Caput 1 De menstruo universali omnium metallorum et mineralium    Duae sunt solutiones; prima cùm materia per materiam primam in materiam   primam redigitur viz: in principia tanquam in siccam quandam aquam quae   non solum ☿  sed et 🜍  dicitur. Et quemadmodum hoc menstruum se habet ad 🜍  eodem modo se gerit   ad 🜍 is  magnetem nempe salem. Et has solutiones fiunt per magica   Elementa ut jam audies.  Caput 2 De Elementis in genere.    Omnia constant ex 4 Elementis et elementa nullibi per se existentia pura et immixta reperiuntur suntque in se invicem  transmutabilia. Caput 3. De Elementis Magicis. i.e. De triplici igne Sapientum antiquorum    Solutio non potest fieri sine his 3 ignibus , Quorum primus metallum in   fluxum adigere debet, secundus sympathiam habet cum igne metallico   estque duplex sive ex duabus contrariis naturis in amicitiam redactis compositus. Itaque pro  simplici sumam. Hic accendit 🜍  metallicum et elementum ignis in metallico   corpore augmentat. Tertius est frigidus metallicus ignis, mercurio ferè   similis. Nam metallum tanquam spiritus pervadit, procurat sympatheticum ignem   in toto penetrare, animam incendit, metallum efficit porosum, principium   est et finis et fundamentum operis: Nam est vehiculum ignis sympathetici et   ab hoc corrumpiter ex sympathia et antipathia.   Quia ignis ardens (i.e. secundus) frigido metallico igni in linea  collaterali cognatus est, estque ignis duplex, habetque cum 🜍 e  et ☿ o  seu serpente   cognationem, vocatur sulphur et nitrum, etc et pro materia prima habendus est   Si hos duos ultimos ignes rectè diagnoscis per quos destructio fieri debet   animamque in igne amburi non permittis et in aerem resolvi tunc   obtentum obtinuisti. Quem admodum per multiplicationem ignis  sympathetici succendisti metallorum ignem et destructionem vel separationem   perfecisti, sic per aliud elementum debet animam efficere corporalem   in aerem abeat. Et hoc fit per terram quae aeri maximè contrariatur quae   non commutatur in aerem nisi prius transeat in aquam, quae est  intermedium elementum. <10.1v> Page Image2    Intellexti quod ☿  tanquam vinculum primo resolutus sit in aliud   elementum, hinc residua solummodo habes duo elementa nempe ignem   et terram. Si vis hæc conservare augmenta Solarem terram i.e solare   corpus: in hoc abscondit se ignis ab illo ardenti sympathetico, quod   ex certo signo potes cognoscere quod signum dum conspicis tunc maxima   sedulitate debes incensam animam cum suo corpore ex igne  liberare alias retines mortuum duntaxat corpus spectabile ad visum ut   Electrum. Promptitudo et dexteritas quae hic requiritur impossibilis   est hic descripta. Tali et non alio modo est anima quasi per  magnetico-metallicum corpus salvanda. Magnes animae sal est, in huius   cassibus praecipua illa duo elementa capiuntur, et ambo haec habent   tertium quoque apud se, sed modo singulari.   Per 🜍  anima et ignis intelligitur, per ☿  spiritus et aer. Et licet ☿  sit astrum omnium ponderosissimum tamen est levissimum et igni ardenti   vult subterfugere super se scandere et in aliud elementum viz in aerem   se resolvere , adeoque iure cum ipso comparatur. Ut enim 🜍  per ignem   intelligitur sic et aer per nitrum et vice versa siquidem in ☿ o    duo elementa ignis et aer potissimum offendantur.   Nitrum et 🜍  amant metalla illisque in linea collaterali cognata   sunt. Videmus enim qualiter spiritus nitri et 🜍 is  metalla invadunt et   in vitriolum convertunt.   Licet Tartarus sit de Regno Vegetabili tamen mineralibus affinis   est, habet cum sale metallorum sympathiam, ei adversatur sal  armoniacum. Metalla meliorat et reddit malleabilia et accordam cum sale   metallico qui per Tartarum multiplicatur nam abundantia salis est   metallorum malleabilitas. Ope Tartari abeunt metalla in vivum ☿  dum nempe   augendo salem disrumpit nexum elementorum.   Mineralis et metallicus ignis per se materia prima est quae  reperitur in minera Saturni ex qua discedere debet prae angustia ignei  volantis draconis qui domicilium frigidi saturni ita incendit ut in eo   mori suumque exhalare spiritum cogatur. Si recipiente possis hunc   spiritum animalum capere habes menstruum universale instar aquae metallicae   Per hunc tantùm anima regis in oleum reducitur. Hic est rectus separator   spiritus vitae spiritus acidus duplex corrosivum, duplici aquae mercuriali similis   et duplici cuidam igneo viro comparatur, estque medium conjugendi animam cum   corpore. Si praedictas ambas (siccam et humidam) solutiones per materiam primam in   materiam adipisci desideras, attende ad qualitates elementorum et sympatheticum  frigidumque metallicum ignem. <10.2r> Page Image5    Caput 4. De metallorum destructione et in tria principia reductione.    Semen metallicum extrahi debet ope astralis universalis seminis quod est   spiritus mercurialis animaque sulphurea de quo ante et post dictum est   Duo tantùm habemus metalla pro opere conficiendo, primum in quo   natura incipit seu semen universale, et ultimum in quo desinit operari   Oportet nos per 🜔  🜍  et ☿  reducere metalla in 🜔  🜍  et ☿    Vitriolum est 🜍 et sal, antimonium vero ☿ , et ex illis crescunt omnia metalla   Separationum optima est haec quando ad summum 🜍  extrahitur per   incensionem magici ignis qui sympathiam habet cum metallo, fitque parvo   tempore igne aperto. Oportet verò unice attendere ne metallorum 🜍    prae incensione sua comburatur. Debet idcirco custos (nempe Terra) adesse   qui hoc impedit . Huius terrae spiritus et Vita Terrae per quem ipsa in visceribus ejus   omnia metalla signit.  Caput 5 De natura primi principii nempe ☿ ii .    Mercurius metallorum communis est humidus et calidus. Per aerem Philosophi hunc ☿ um  intelligunt   est instar nitri cui tanquam ignis seu spiritus prae aliis addictus est. Habet multùm 🜍 is  et nihil salis   Apropriatur in specie pulmoni propter elementum aeris quo pulmo carere nequit. Penetrat   ambo alia principia sal et 🜍  seu Corpus et animam et conjungit gestat enim animam in ventre   suo. Si sal non ei adjungitur penetrat et solvit Tartarum et in morbis ex viscoso Tartaro ortis unice  usurpatur. Sed hic non est Philosophorum sed metallorum qui parum prodest in opere maiori nisi praeparetur.  Caput 6 De secundo principio 🜍    Sulphura sunt omnia volatilia ignea et concremabilia nisi illis arte succursum   sit. Sulphura Metallica resistunt corruptioni praesertim si sale fortificantur.  Caput 7 De tertio principio 🜔    Sal et conservatio et induratio omnium rerum. Et metallorum malleabilitas   et prae inopia salis ipsa sunt immalleabilia, hoc testatur   qui plurimae ex   parte sal est, ut calcinatio ejus testatur. Quicquid ☿  et 🜍  in medicina praestant id   sal meliorare aggreditur, humanum 🜍  spiritumque vitalem conservat et adversus omnes morbos defendit  Caput 8. An Medicina universalis sit in 7 astris id est in 7 metallis   an verò in auro et materia prima    Anima mundi est Menstruum universale per quod medicina incipitur et   finitur hoc reperimus in omnibus metallis fusis, praecipuè verò in   non fusis. Medicina universalis nedum in omnibus metallis conjunctim   verum in auro solo et ejus magnete consistit. In dupliciter  armato saturno (sic dictus quia proprium 🜍  secum habet) latet suprema medicina, praecipuè quando 🜍  et 🜔  ex   auro est extractum, quia hoc solum continet omnium planetarum   virtutes et naturam.   Defectu verò auri potest medicina ex omnibus metallis conjunctim   fieri, nempe ex earum 🜍  et 🜔 e  per sympatheticum metallicum ignem  extractis, qui incendendo 🜍  metalli, mercurialem ligaturam dissolvit.   Ubi talem animam per magica elementa comparasti in ea solve   suum salem et spiritum mundi ex anima mundi; tunc spiritus proprio  sanguine tingitur et sale fortificatur. Mars per illud quod in seipso est redigendus est in materiam primam .   Habet solare sulphur et sal valde maturatum . Qui Martem vincet muliebri ♀ is  argutia circumducet, sed oportet ipsam inverti   et tunc veterem formam reliquit et cor calidum illius lasciviens est instar   cordis frigidi recani Saturni quando primum terrestris arietis gradum  ingressus est et una falce orientem aliaque occidentem versus, ferit et messem   facit. Faelix   es si noris venerem hanc recte praeparare, nempe debes illam in minerale  redigere et illud in aquam mercurialem et exinde clarum quoddam oleum   conficere, hoc cum astrali sale custodi nam in eo est virtus generativa.   Mars sagittam armis gestat clypeoque suo equestrem lanceam quod  denotat heroum ortum et nobilem prosapiam.   Cardo rei est reductio in materiam primam seu tria principia. Estque   praecipuum punctum ad universale generalissimum. Unde dicunt, Aurum faciliùs   fieri quam corrumpi. Ego autem hoc praestare possum trium aurorarum  curriculo idque per materiam primam. Ope solutionis deprehendimus anaticam proportionem principiorum auri .   Conjunctio principiorum auri interea fit dum cunctos supremum astrum   planetas per spiritum mundi transmeavit, et alias non. Et sicut neque   particulare nec universale absque spiritu mundi (vice cuius est spiritus  saturnialis duplicati mercurii usurpandus) confici nequeat, sic universale  generalissimum extra tria exordia auri non possumus affabrè construere   Sol omnibus planetis splendorem communicat, et e contra ab illis ex  utroque latere < or >utraque parte    juvatur fortificatur et incenditur. Caput 14 De natura fugitivi ☿ ii .    Visibilem ☿ um  effice invisibilem, depone a dignitate et praecipita infra   se, redige in terram unde natus est, et multo maiorem calorem quam ante   poterit sustinere, ostendetque tibi materiam prirmam, clavem operis. Per <10.3v> Page Image detractionem honoris capitisque elati praecipitationem commutatus est in frigidum   toxicum, perque infamiam et exitium exaltatur ad maximam dignitatem   Oportet verò sibi ab ipso cavere quia est venenosior quàm ante Ex   sanguine ejus distillamus lac virginis quod cum sanguine igniti leonis
