  abit et non aliter. Unde inconstans sibi ☿ us  de amabilitate Veneris   congratulatur. Sed sisto, nam hoc est secretum sapientum sigillum .Quare   relinquo tibi ☿ um  ad examinandum cum proprietate Venereas.  Caput 15. De natura Phlegmaticae Lunae.    Luna concordat ex parte cum Saturno, ille supremus et haec   extremus est Planeta, uterque a Sole aeque longè distant.   Nihil solare potest de luna extrahi nisi quod in ipsam priùs introductum   fuerit per specialem Arsenicalem ingressum. Hoc autem pertinet ad   universale et non fit nisi per Philosophorum Alcahestum   fitque via sicca vel humida ut credo te ex praecedentibus intellexisse.  Caput 16. De Gemmis, quomode nempe ad sublimem medicinam per   universale Menstruum reducuntur.    Idem spiritus mundi (quem  Basilius  vocat omnia in omnibus )   etiam gemmas pervadit et virtutes extrahit. Granatus est Saturnalis   Topazius Jovialis, Rubinus Martialis, Carbunculus Solaris est imò aurum  virtute excellit, et 🜍  ejus cordi maiorem adfert fortitudinem quam 🜍  Solis.   Smaragdus est Venereus, et Crystallus Martialis [forte mercurialis].  Mercurio affinis est Saturnus sicut et crystallo Granatus. Mercurius nempe saturni   e divite quadam nondum fusa minerali educitur ope Aquilae et Draconis   qui alias etiam Rupicoluber dicitur, quod totum idem est habens diversa tantùm   nomina. Saphirus est lunaris et medetur cerebri imfirmitatibus Sed corpus   priùs aperi debet et inde nobilis anima sive 🜍  et Sale educi per spiritum Saturnialem   (qui e saccaro aut potius mercurio saturni ope salis ammoniaci  educitur) et fieri potabilis.   Comparatur sal Ammoniacum aquilae propter volatilitatem et sulphur Draconi propter   voracitatem nam peredit et calcinat omnia corpora. Ex utrisque   his spiritibus ope Vulcani grave oleum extrahitur quod omnia metallica   et mineralia sulphura solvit et in oleum resolvit. Iam aures arrige   Si ejus partem unam super duas purae separatae argenti calcis proiicias figit se   oleum illud simul cum metallico 🜍 e  in Luna, calefacit ipsam evehitque   ad naturam solarem. Et si possit Draco solus tam habilem reddere ☿ um  quid non alba Aquila praestabit. <10.4r> Page Image Granatus et Plumbum sanant defectus spleneticos seu lienis.  Topazius et Jupiter sanant Iecuris defectus. Rubinus et Mars curant  defectus omnes a bile ortos. Carbunculus et Sol Cordis defectibus medentur  3    Smaragdus et Venus morbis renum et Vesicae (nempe sal ♀ is  per 🜈   clarificatus). Mercurius (i.e. ♁ ij ) & Crystallus medentur pulmonibus. Lunaque et   Sapphirus Cerebro.  Caput 17. De Medicina Vegetabilium et Animalium, quae sympathicè   ad primam medicinam spectat.    Homo vinum et aurum sibi invicem benevola sunt. Vinum est vehiculum   universalis ac particularis medicinae, imò non tantum est vegetabile menstruum   sed et minerale. Talem habet amicitiam qualem ardens quoddam 🜍  cum 🜍 e    metallico unde venit, ut aurum potabile sine illo nequeat fieri. Spiritus ☿ ii  extrahit tincturam de 🜍 e  solis, tanquam intimum ex anima et spiritus   vini extrahit intimum ex his intimis, faecibus iterum separatis quae   ad tincturam non pertinent. Tinctura est siccum 🜍  quod per ☿   philosophorum saturnialem seu animam mundi ita dissolvitur ut nullo modo sejungi   possint. Et spiritum vini similiter esse verum menstruum solutivum constat in   auro potabili: Quia nec a primò menstruo nec ab extractione magis  separari potest quam aqua ab aqua. Rectè itaque dicunt solvens a soluto non   posse divelli sed debet cum eo duodecim dies minimùm in digestione stare   rubramque effigiem prae se ferre, modo aliàs verum aurum potabile dicendum   erit. Et cum 🜈  sit vegetabile menstruum medicina etiam vegetabilis est.   Haud aliter quoque Tartarus est sublime menstruum et medicina,   cuius spiritus ad reclusionem metallorum potest usurpari. Nam favet   metallis augens eorum malleabilitatem i.e. salem, quia Sal est metallorum   malleabilitas. Unde possumus tartarum vel satius spiritum ejus ad  separandum tria principia usurpare ut supra indicavi. Nam cum sal ejus   ope sic augmentatum incipit dominari caetera duo principia cedunt et   sequitur separatio. Sed non est via nec intentio ut sulphur deperdamus.   In Tartaro praeter clarificatum salem occultatur tinctura quae calculum   aliosque morbos, mediante vehiculo suo, dispellit. Ex tartaro ope 🜍 is   liquoreum quoddam menstruum seu oleum distillatur quod tincturas et  essentias ex omnibus vegitabilibus attrahit, praecipuè dum spiritus calcis vivae   ipsi incorporatus est. Ab hoc autem menstruo potest 🜈  de integro auferre   menstruo illaeso manente. Ex his ambobus igneis spiritibus oleum   quoddam extrahitur quod omne naturae suae dissolvit et consumit, quia in ipso 
  sunt serpens et Draco simul. Item ex Dracone solo per additionem  Draconis volantis quem Philosophi sal ammoniacum 
  vocant elicitur perigneus et misticus spiritus. Gratulor illum qui hunc 
  spiritum cum sui simili animare novit. Sed ad tartarum redeo. 
  Tinctura Tartari obtinetur sic. Calcina sal Tartari tam diu in igne, 
  donec color optatus apparuerit, Huic affunde rectificatum 🜈  qui tincturam 
<10.4v> Page Image4  instar sanguinis rubicundissimi extrahet. Haec potes separare vel  conjuncta servare pro lubitu, mihi sufficit 
  indicasse qua ratione in vegetabili regno 
  consimilis insignis medicina et Menstruum possit obtineri. 
  Et cùm illud menstruum ex Animali regno etiam tincturas et 
  essentias extrahere potest, de Animalibus sufficit praeterea observare 
  quod sicut in Auro solo virtus aliòrum omnium metallorum absconditur, sic 
  in corde humano reperitur suprema medicina. Inque corde vino et 
  auro sunt omnes omnium rerum virtutes.  Caput: 18. De Alphabeto Chymico seu veterum signis et characteribus.    Dixi de Materia prima, de menstruo et medicina universali, deque magicis 
  elementis sive clavibus quantùm licuit clarè. Iam Abecedarium veterum 
  discutiam et novos aliquos characteres ad modum significantes adiungam. 
  Menstruum philosophicum aceto acerrimo assimulatur, itemque vitriolo 
  sive nitro. Saturnus est lunaris ut, demonstrat ejus character qui est 
  semiluna. Sapientum saturnus est solaris et insigniri deberet charactere 
  solis nempe circulo qui est figura perfectissima. Cùm sit origo  omnium metallorum signum ejus deberet omnium characteri respondere sed 
  sapientes speciali consideratione solummodo per signum vitrioli nitri aceti 
  saturni sive mercurii expresserunt. 
  Qui haec in unum formare characterem harmonice novit habet 
  materiam ante oculos. Signum aceti sive  Azothi  primas habebit  deinde quae aceto proxime cognata sunt pedibus insequentur  
  Sub vitriolo 🜍  ardens intelligitur quod aliquando dicimus igneum Draconem 
  Qui frigidum Draconem intelligit nihilo ulteriùs eget quam rubrum   quendam igneum Draconem huic fratri suo adjungere, tunc duas  naturas in unam redegit, quae tamen ante unica fuere et in  qualitatibus tantum diversificabantur, retinent vero ambae unam spiritualem 
  mercurialem naturam. Absque hoc nequit universale generalissimum 
  confici Deberet characteri Altissimi consignari (nempe circulo) quia Deus omnium rerum  virtutes ei indidit, Ab hac materia proveniunt sanitas et divitiae. 
  Nihil refert sive globum hujus mundi ut circulum sive ut salem et nitrum 
  expendas. Non autem debemus cum his procedere ut supra dictum est sed 
  haec duo bene permiscebis, proprio suo aceto imbibes ita ut in quantitate 
  materiae supernatet. Acetum autem fieri oportet ex praedictis salibus 
  per diametrum salis seu vitrioli et perpendiculum nitri, sic fit acetum 
  acerrimum. Per crucem saepe intelligitur Azoth seu acetum nempe  redemptor qui se de sursum in hunc mundum deorsum demisit, ferturque ut 
  spiritus super aquam salis seu vitrioli et nitri ex quo ipse natus et  extractus est.  <10.5r> Page Image extractus est.   Principiumet finis lapidis est duplex caudatus saturnus. Hae caudae sunt   lunariae et per specialem conjunctionem caelestemque figuram in primo et ultimo   tactu lunae ad huiusmodi arduam virtutem ac heroricum characterem  transmutatae sunt. Hoc in se complectitur totum Alphabetum chemicum. Nam in   hoc supremus saturnus maximè omnium visibilis est. Mars ex hoc charactere   dignoscitur, sed impones illi cassidem suum, tunc praesentat   Mars officia sua, designat principium, materiamque primam in Terra et in  minera sua expressè demonstrat, quoniam ille alioquin libenter ibi est.   Mercurius est vera Venus quae lunarem coronam gestat et cum illa   solare 🜍  habeat, discupit illam aequus bonusque Jupiter ad maiorem   exaltare honorem. Extrahe mercurio cornua, id est, ambure ipsi alas   suas, sic est venus quaedam, invertas et habes efficax quoddam 🜍 , quia   Vitriolum est 🜍  et Antimonium ☿ .   Mercurius habet sub pedibus salutiferum signum quod  Moyses  in deserto erexit   et serpentem affixit. Debet crucem devorare, stomacho concoquere, seque   statuto tempore cruce cibare et sustentare. Spolia ipsum ornamento   capitis sic sphaericus est truncus super quem fortuna circumcursitat donec   ipsi nutrimentum rude et indigestum ultra se superius erumpat. Tunc est   perquam submissus et humilis quasi redigisses in vitriolum per proprium   acetum quod ipsi subjectum est. Qui hunc novit faelix est. Adige  mercurium in crystallum, adde ipsi salem suum, sic habes Universale. Alphabetum simplex

Appendix brevis, seu praecedentium    Corruptio fit quando aliquod Elementum corroboratur ut caeteris praevaleat   et opprimat. Qui ex hac corruptione scit metallicum 🜍  per magicum ignem   capere is gloriam huius mundi expugnavit. Ignis hic est duplex. Et  quemadmodum illa duo elementa duos suos habent hostes qui corruptionem efficiunt   Sic sapientes duo elementa vel potiùs. Elementorum receptacula habent per   quae unum aut alterum Elementum augmentant, fortificant, aliaque opprimunt.   Magi in resolutione metallorum utuntur duobus contrariis   elementis quae duos ignes nominavi, nempe sympatheticum flagrantem Hermaphrodiicum ignem, et frigidum metallicum ignem. elementum igneum seu   metallorum anima est 🜍  eorum; frigidus ignis est ☿  qui metallorum aqua 
  dicitur et per salem reducitur.   Vulgare Sulphur et nitrum sunt ambo efficaces ignes sed infestissimi  inimici, si scis hos reconciliare, et tum metallicum 🜍  per illorum igneum   spiritum incendere propositum obtinuisti.   Possumus per multiplicationem salis metalla destruere per mineralem   et Tartarium salem ut supra dixi, at non conducit ad obtinendum 🜍  seu animam metallorum. Ista anima capi debet per illum ex  contrariis constructum ignem quae 🜍  et nitrum dicuntur,   Ut etiam notitiam habeas frigidi metallici ignis, scito eum esse ☿ um   saturni qui se cum metallis amalgamat, et ope jam praedicti ignei duplicis   elementi in igne se patitur calcinari.   Jam dilucidissimam doctrinam a me praecepisti, Philosophi duo tantum  Elementa venerantur viz: ignem et aquam, seu ☿  et 🜍  quorum utrumque  inimicum habet. Ex his duobus mediante sale unumquodque in elementatum abiit   et per eadem iterum destruuntur. Haec sunt elementa magica quae omnia   corrumpunt. Estque haec sicca prima solutio de qua prolixius in  principio tractavi <10.6r> Page Image Altera humida solutio fit per ☿ um  Saturni quem animam Mundi   vocavi et spiritum ejus spiritum mundi siquidem ab hoc omnia metalla   oriuntur.  11    Philosophi non tantum duo habent elementa Magica, Mercurios duos   et Saturnos duos, verum etiam duo duntaxat metalla   Differt ☿ us  vulgi a mercurio Philosophorum, ille habet semen argenti et   calidus est, hic habet semen auri estque frigidus metallicus mercurius, & Solaris   magnes, sicut et ille dici potest lunaris magnes, et hoc ita esse designat   mercurius sua semiluna quacum illum sapientes insignierunt ut a   solari discriminarent. Hos duos mercurios dignoscimus ex fumo qui est   illorum sulphur. Oportet animadvertere quàm sint fixi aut fugitivi ,   et citò vel tardè sublimentur et praecipitantur, quo meliùs ab invicem   distinguamus.   Et quemadmodum Natura duos generavit mercurios, solarem et  lunarem , sic ex duobus illis fixis corporibus similis mercurius praeparari   possit. Imò ex mediis metallis ut Antimonio et Bismutho sive  Arsenico fiunt vivi mercurii qui unicè ad medicinam conducunt. Hi   tamen pro ☿ o  Philosophorum non sunt censendi cum ille ☿ us  sit   menstruum universale et bis de ☿ o  natus primò de lunari postea   solari, Et merito dicitur duplex mercurius.   Lunaris mercurius leviori opera ex Saturnino corpore educitur ac   distillatur habetque naturam mercurii Lunae, nam per saturnum Luna   generatur. Et pariter de minera Veneris calidus solaris ☿ ius  extrahitur   per tartarum et salem ammoniacum, quae (viz tartarus et 🜹 ) licet summè inimica sint   tamen hoc negotium promovere debent. Qui novit ☿  frigidi saturni   et ☿ um  calidae veneris in oleum redigere is habet Menstruum  Universale, firmamque clavem adeptus est qua omnia 🜍 a  reddat potabilia ; 
  sed ad rem.   Philosophi sub duplici mercuriali spiritu intelligunt praefatos duos   Saturnos ex quibus uterque ille ☿ ius  et postea eorum spiritus extrahitur   ac distillatur. Hisce tantum duobus metallis Philosophi antiqui ad magnum   mysterium usi fuêre.   Primum et ultimum metallum Philosophi pro uno habent Primum est   sperma metallorum in minera saturni repertum, radix metallorum, plumbum   nostrum, Azoth de quo Lac virginis destillamus, habetque veneream proprietatem   Ultimum metallum est aurum. Ars parvo tempore ex hoc solari compacto <10.6v> Page Image12  corpore translucidum quoddam corpus efficit  quam  Philosophi  vitrificationem vocant. Hoc est nobilissimum quod natura et Ars perficit,  Quomodo haec vitrificatio fit nimis longum esset dicere, tamen hanc   accipe instructionem. ℞  igneum elementum magicum ex infernalibus contrariis materiis 🜍 e  et nitro compositum. Cum hoc igne incipe in fine   naturae, nempe oppugna et calcina aurum alias incremabile, sic incendit   unus ignis alium viz unum 🜍  aliud. Sic elementum ignis seu 🜍  animave auri   in illo dominatur, quod ubi contingit, debet elementum aqueum, nempe  humidus fugitivus ☿ , ad suum astrum volare; et cum ille mercurius sit vinculum corporis   et animae, unde sequitur separatio animae a corpore. Debet verè ad hoc   alter ille frigidus metallicus ignis etiam usurpari qui de minerali  nondum fuso et immalleabili saturno extrahitur. Hic saturnialis ☿ ius  non ardet ut  communis ☿ ius  sed habet in se terrestram siccam qualitatem quacum potest 🜍    solis defendere et praeservare ne comburatur et cum suo ☿ o  avolet   Quando Aurum cum ☿ o  amalgamatum est, efficitur porosum,  potestque infernalis ignis solidum illud corpus multo citiùs et melius  calcinare et solarem ignem ab omni parte accendere inque cineres redigere.   Ex hoc deducimus cum claro caelico rore sulphur, et ex remanente   corpore elixiviamus post debitam reverberationem pretiosissimum  medicinalem salem, de quo Philosophi dicunt Sal metallorum est Lapis  Philosophorum. Hic sal cum spiritu vini clarificari debet, dein cum   suo sulphure unitur et eum spiritu mundi ex mercurio Saturni distillato   imbibitur inque unico vitro et fornace putrificatur et figitur, et post  fermentatur et pro lubitu multiplicatur. Sic fit universalis medicina.   Sic perspicuè te instruxi sed non clarè docui quomodo ex  immaleabili non fuso minerali saturno sublimis ☿  extrahitur, et quomodo is   praeterea in rubicundum spiritum adigitur, Id quod fit unica tantùm via   et medio. Imò non tantùm adaptationem frigidi metallici ignis celavi   sed et praeparationem igneae Magicae clavis ex infernalibus ignibus viz 🜍 e    nitro unitis qui licet sibi mutuo adversantur tamen uniri debent, et uterque   tunc unum praestant effectum.   Scrupulus restat adimendus circa praefatos duos ☿ os  seu saturnos <10.7r> Page Image13  Audivisti quod ex luna et etiam frigido arietino Saturno mercurius potest   fieri. Item quod ex minera  Veneris  solarem quendam ☿ ium  liceat   efficere qui solari 🜍 e  praeditus est, unde ☿ um  solis dixi. Tamen   ipse ☿ ium  Saturni ad destructionem auri usus sum. Scias itaque ☿ ium    rubrum in Lunari alba tinctura maiori cum commodo usurpari   posse. Et sicut ad extrema non itur nisi per Media sic ad rubram   tincturam non devenitur nisi habita prius alba. Sic potes ipse tibi   scupulum adimere, et recolere quod hic non de universali  generalissimo sed de simplici universali scripserim.   Universale generalissimum ex duplici ☿ o  ut supra dixi   producitur et cum solari 🜍 e  animatur et fermentatur et cum   perdurante auri sale figitur nec non ulterius in infinitum per   duo alia sulphura augetur in quantitate et qualitate. Qualitas per 🜍  cibatur, quantitas augetur per jam dictum ☿ um  qui habet  veneream proprietatem et naturam mineralis frigidi saturni. Ideoque duplici ☿ o  assimulatur duplatusque dicitur. Nam habet qualitatem Veneris   ratione calidi 🜍 is , et figiditatem ex parte Saturni.   In caput 13. Venus habet influentiam in renes. Estque 🜍  ♀ is  ut et ♂ is

Vitrificatum et fixatum venus pellit   tantum sudorem . Valentines 12 Keys.    Proem. Seek not the stone in things combustible as vegetable & Animalls,   every thing springs from its one seed, of mans seed nothing is generated but man   & to generate metalls is required a Metalline seed which seed springs from a metalline   root, & is that which worketh metalls in the mine. God at first created the seeds of all   things & man hath no power to make any new seed. pag 2. 3. 4.   Metalls consist of ☿ , 🜍 , & 🜔 , Soul, spirit, & body. And without a Soul there   is no perfection. Spirits hidden in metalls are some more volatile then others, &   their souls & bodies also differ. ☉  & ☽  are fixed, ♀  is almost all tincture. ♂  his   body is hard & sturdy but his valour with ☽ s  fixation & ♀  beauty in a mixture   harmonize etc pag 5, 6 & 7   Afterward follows the work in a Parable pag 8,9, 10 & 11.   Key 1. Impure things are unfit for the work. Wares out of Mines are worth their   money, but if sophisticated they are made unfit being adulterated in their originall  operation.   Take the greedy gray wolf which by its name is subject to ♂  but by descent is  s  child, found in Vallys & hills of the wood & is very hungry, let him devour the King   then burn him & the king will be set at liberty. Doe thus thrice & the King will be   cleansed.   Keep the fountain from muddiness, & if our body be broken by a corrosity, let the   corrosiveness be washed away.   Key 2.  Apollo  & Diana when they are to be married must have severall garments made for them & be well washt with waters made by severall distillings   which differ much according to their severall uses. Some water in their preparation   must be oft distilled, & the abstracts restored to the earth & drawn of again. But  Apollo  &  Dian  must be espoused naked their ornaments & cloathing being taken of   that their seed be not destroyed by strange mixtures.   The Bridegrooms Bath must be made of two contrary waters, the eagle & the   cold Dragon which must one conquer the other. The Dragons fiery spirit  burns  the eagles   feathers, & dissolves him to make him a fit bath for the King.   Key. 3. Our fiery 🜍  must be prepared with water, to which the 🜍  gives strength   & vertue to destroy it & make it invisible, after which it appears again triumphant   with great melioration of its condition. This 🜍  must its self be exalted before it can   exalt others. Seek it in subject where it is incombustible which cannot be unless   the sea-salt swallow the body & cast it up again, then exalt it that it excell in   lustre & scarlet blood red colour.   Key 4. Calcination must be made into ashes but of which the Phaemnix[i.e. Sal Albrot] shall arise   again more glorious. In these ashes is a salt of great efficacy. But it availes nothing   unlesse turned inside outwards, & its spirit set loos. <11.2v> Page Image4    Key. 5. In the Earth is a spirit from which all Vegetables Metalls & Mineralls   receiv their increase & nourishment. Our gold hath a Magnet which is the materia   prima of our stone. From this matter must be expelled a spirit, which is the root of   life, & ☿  of the Philosophers. Our matter in the beginning must be purified,   opened, broaken, destroyed & reduced to dust, & then a volatile spirit made of it as   white as snow & another volatile spirit as red as blood, these two contein a third   & yet are but one spirit.   Key 6. Observe just proportions of your materialls. A double fiery man must   be fed with a white swan; & thre parts of the glass must be empty. Two men   can master a third. A double wind must come called Vulturnus & their a single   one called Notus etc.   Key 7. Keep a good regimen of fire, which must be increased at the latter end   Key 8. All generation is from putrefactionof the seed, nor can any mettalline seed   work & be augmented untill of it self without any strange addition or mixtur 
  it be brought into a full putrefaction. These metalls must reach their perfect   operations by the help of the 4 Elements, not that the Elements are the metalline seed   but that seed must by them be further brought to putrefaction.   Note also that the spirit of wine riseth before the flegm, but if by further heat or  putrefaction it be turned to vinegre its spirit riseth last.   Key 9. Saturn is the cheif key of the Art & is set on the lowest step. His  corruption must be the way for his melioration passing from black to white & Red through   the colours of all the other Planets.   Key 10. Our stone conteins in it the vertues of all things in the world. Make   not too much hast in the work but guide the fire regularly by a graduated heat   till you attain your wish.   Key 11. Multiplication is thus.  Orpheus  married his sister  Euridice  & that he   might have children by her hee tooke the blood out of his right side & her left side   & the blood which did stick in the hearts of his father & Mother & inclosed them in   a glass.   Key 12. Ferment the medicine with well purified gold before you project. The third part of B. Valentines  Testament , being an Elucidation of the   twelve Keys.    From mixture of a heavenly influence Elementall operation & earthly   substance arise the 4 Elements which by the help of fire hid therein a warm   digestion producee a soul spirit & body. The 3 principles in coagulation come to a ☿  🜍  & 🜔  & these in conjunction according to the nature of the seed produce a perfect   body: bee it in the animall vegetable or minerall Kingdom pag 116. <11.3r> Page Image5    Metalls that they may be propagated must be reduced to their first seed or   materia prima, their Metalline form must be destroyed & separated into a ☿  🜍  & 🜔 , which separation is done in & by a mercurial spirit, sulphureous soul   & white salt. And these thre must be duly joyned again that they may be  brought, to the highest & most perfect purity. p 116 & 117.   All metall & mineralls have onely one root from which their descent is, in   which one thing all these three, spirit, soul, & body ly hid. Learn this golden seed or Magnet   In my 12 Keys I told you that the stone may be made out of 🜍  & 🜔  with the help of the   spirit of ☿  which must be drawn from a crude unmelted minera according to my   fifth Key. pag 117 & 118.   The first time I made the stone out of purified ☉  alledged in the first chapter   & this way I declared in my 12 Keys. But afterwards I took into further  consideration the tinging animated spirits planted in their severall bodies & have here   pointed at a gold which is much better. videlicet In a more known despicable minerall  substance is found a 🜍  & tincture more effectuall & more worthy then the best   gold can afford, which is fluid & open & its ☿ all  spirit also & mysticall salt is free   & open by which the Mastery may be obteined in shorter time & more easily.   On the first Key. If you work with ☉  you must purge it well with ♁  thrice, & then drive of the Regulus with  .   On the second& third Key. The Kings Balneum is made of the Dragon & Eagle which   after their union are made into an aquafort into which the King is thrown   & by digestion for 14 days corroded of this salt water into a most splendant transparent yellow oyle. Distill   it all thrice in a retort & then abstract the water in Balneo Mariae & you will have a gold   pouder. Iterate the solution distillations & evaporation & from the remaining calx   or chrystalls of ☉  extract the tincture with spirit of salt precipitate it with ☿  vive, evaporate the ☿  & calcine the remaining pouder keep this for conjunction etc p 121. 122.   Saturn will put into thy hand a deep glittering Minera which in his Myne is   grown of the first mater of all metalls. With the water of this is the gold seed   brought to putrefaction & volatility & coagulated into a new clarifyed body which   will never part for they are of one nature & have their birth from this volatil   bird. But these Minerall spirits are in other Metalls also, The golden 🜍  is onely   in red metalls but the magnetick power is in its white mercuriall spirit, which   binds the soul & dissolveth the body. Therefore the astrum of Sol is not onely in gold   that with the spirit of ☿  & solar 🜔  onely the stone may be made but may be also   prepared out of copper & steel which have red tinging qualities as well as gold.  pag 127 , 128 , 130, 131, 134,    The afforesaid white spirit is the ☿  of the Philosophers the Key & primum   mobile. Of this spirit fed by 1131-3r_01  with a metallin 🜍  & 🜔  may be made a matter not unlike   the great stone. pag 118, 128, 142   Goldish sulphurs are found most effectuall in ♂  & ♀  which may be reduced to a most   effectuall vitrioll out of which in short time the 3 principles (🜔  🜍  & ☿ ) may easily be  separated & these thre are also hid in a minerall vitrioll as well as in the minerall <11.3v> Page Image itself. Of which Vitriol the best sort is found in  Hungary  like a blew saphire  6 
  pag 128, 130, 132, 133   Calcine this vitrioll & distill the white spirit of in a well coated Retort & there   will remain in the bottom a red oyle ponderous as gold or lead & thick as blood   of a burning fiery quality which is our fluid gold. pag 134.   Vitriol in distillation yeilds first a white spirit & then a red oyle p 143. & from   these you must expect again a white spirit, an of a red quality & christalline salt.   which 3 joyned generate the stone. p 143. see also pag 144 & 145 & 146.   In ♂  & ♀  is found the root of the red tincture much more then in ☉   itself p 144   For a further explication of the first six keys about preparing gold for   conjunction, & of the preparations & severall species of salts, & mercuries or menstruums   See the short way & repetition, & the treatis of the Philosophers 🜍  🜖  &   magnet & the Sulphur; of Sol in the fourth part of the Testament. Also of the stellate Regulus of ♂  , & of the metalline salt which turneth   to water permanent, & is the Philosophers magnet which loveth its philosophick   mars. The Fifth part of B. Valentines last will & Testament    The highest aurum potabile & most universall medicine for man in nature is the prepared excocted &   fixed substance of our stone before tis fermented, & this after fermentation is the   most universall medicine for metalls. pag 3.   There may be another aurum potabile made of well purified gold thus   extract its soul with the sweet spirit of common salt, which will leave the   remaining body very white. Abstract the spirit of salt from the anima edulcorate it   ten or 12 times, exiccate it well, weigh it, pour on it 4 times as much   spirit of ☿ , putrify it in Balneo Mariae gently till the anima be quite dissolved &   turned to water or materia prima passing first through various colours till it   become a blood red transparent liquor without feces in the bottom,pour on   it twice as much well rectified spirit of wine, digest it 12 or 15 days more, abstract   it per Alembicum, iterate the abstraction that nothing corporeall be left in the bottom   so have you a true Aurum potabile of a blood red transparent colour which   can never be reduced into a body. pag 3 & 4.   A half Aurum potabile may be prepared out of the said Anima Auri by   using well rectified oyle of Vitriol, or much better by using the philosophick 🜍  or oyle (which is the second Principle drawn with spirit of ☿  from the Philosophick earth)  instead of the spirit of ☿ . pag 4 & 5   In like manner with the sky coloured 🜍  of ☽  & spirit of ☿  which is praepared from the white spirit of 🜖  may be   made argentum potabile. <11.4r> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <11.4v> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <11.5r> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <11.5v> Page Image<This page is blank except for possible shelfmarks and indications of library ownership.>  <11.6r> Page Image11b  7   B. Valentine s 12 Keys.    Proem. Every thing groweth from it proper seed, therefore to make metalls take that   seed of which nature makes them in the mine   Metalls consist of spirit soul & body 🜍  ☿  & 🜔 , & without a soul there is no  perfection.   See the work described in a Parable.   Key 1. Let nothing impure enter the work; therefore cast your gold through ♁  thrice   & then drive of the ♁  with  ; also let the heterogeneous corrosives with which you afterward break & separate this Gold into its   principles be wholly washt away again.   Key 2. Amalgam this ☉  with ☿ , Dissolve & Volatize the amalgama with aqua Regis   made of saltpeter & Salarmoniack (dragon & eagle) or better of saltpeter & common salt   & when the body is sufficiently broken abstract the liquor in Balneo Mariae gently. etc dry the calx   Key .3 On that calx pour spirit of sea-salt to extract the tincture or 🜍 , & the remaining body will be a white calx. evaporate the water, & there will   remain a red pouder, the kings purple mantle, or 🜍  of ☉    Key. 4. Calcine well the remaining white calx, by a reverberatory fire & out of that Calx with spirits of   common or sea salt & wine incorporated extract the solar salt. This salt availes   nothing unles its inside be turned outward & its spirit set loos by digestion with spirit of ☿    Key 5. Digest this 🜍  & 🜔  of ☉  with spirit of ☿  apart, untill each of them   be (after putrefaction) inseperably united with it, the 🜍  becoming a volatile spirit as   red as blood & the salt another volatile spirit as white as snow. Then joyne   these two spirits together   Key. 6 The spirit of ☿  must be in just proportion. & 3 parts of the glass must   be empty. 
  Key 7 Digest these in a graduated heat till they be fixt.   Key 8 They will then putryfy together & afterwards rise again glorified   Key 9. Black must be turned to white & white to red. The regimens or planets   succeeding in order.   Key 10 Make not too much hast in the regimens but expect with patience & at last the   King will arize triumphant & invincible. our red & ponderous stone   Key 11. This is multiplyed with the mercuriall spirit etc   Key. 12 Ferment the stone before you project. which Fermentation is done thus. Take as   much fine gold as of your stone set them in a crucible in first a gentle heat for 12 howers, then   let them be in continuall melting for 3 days & nights. <11.6v> Page Image8   Things remarkable in  B. Valentine s works.  

 1. That common Gold may by heterogeneous corrosives duely prepared be separated   into 🜍  🜔  & ☿ . 

 2. That this solar 🜍  & 🜔  may be digested with the mercuriall spiritof ♁  to an Elixir. Which  intimates that that spirit is substantially ☿  because substituted in liew of the ☿  of ☉ . 

 3. That the white body of ☉  which conteins the 🜔  & ☿  after the 🜍  is abstracted may   be digested with philosophick 🜍  or red oyle (extracted with the spirit of ☿ ) so as to becom ☉  again. Which shows that oyle to be of the same substance with the anima or 🜍  of ☉    becaus substituted in its stead. And consequently the fixed substance remaining after   the spirit & oyle are abstracted is of the substance of the 🜔  of ☉ . p 155 

 4 That as in the preparation of the Philosophick ☿  so here the 🜔  may be digested   putrified in 40 days & turned inside out with the spirit of ☿ , coagulating after blackness volatility& many colours into new   body as white as snow pag 136, 128 145 146.  

 5 That the 🜍  of ☉  & spirit of ☿  cannot be digested to a metall without the 🜔 .   but resolve into a spirituall potable red   oyle. So that 🜔  is the principle of coagulation or humidum radicale p 129

Note that this but like the great work p 142 

 6. That he calls this white body the white elixir p 136

 9 That of the 🜍  & 🜔  of ☽  with spirit of ☿  may be made the white stone   & multiplyed.  10. That many particular tinctures may be made by joyning the 🜍  of some   bodys with the calces of others & fermenting them with spirit of ☿ . But without the spirit   of ☿  there is nothing done.

 12. As in the body of 🜖  is spirit oyle & 🜔  so in that spirit is also spirit oyle   & salt & if you can separate this spirit of 🜖  as it ought it affords you again thre principles out of which  1141-4v_01    without any addition the stone hath ever been made since the beginning of the world. p 143 144 

 13 The spirit of ☿  is had out of the Magnet, & this conteins <11.7r> Page Image9  its 3 principles as was said. p 144. 118 And affords a better tincture then ☉  p 119   How tis made p 127 

 15. That in ♂  & ♀  & ♁  is the root of the red tincture more powerfully then in   gold its selfe. p 144, 130. 138. 128 Also Cur: Triumph Antimonii p 42. 

 14. That the Magnet is a deep glittering unmelted minerall, Saturns ofspring, subjet   by name to ♂ , conteining the matter of which all metalls are made which is a  mercuriall spirit made up of three principles, is the golden seed etc p 117, 118, 119, 127 & Key 1   & this is Antimony; compare pag 12 with Key 1. 

 16. Regarde onely metalline vitriolls & note thatof ♂  & ♀  may be made an   excellent vitriol in which all thre principles ly hid. The same are also in a   minerall vitriol of which the best sort is found in  Hungary . p 132, 133. 146.   That vitrioll alone is sufficient to make the stone p 122 which no one thing besides can doe 

 17. That every thing springs from its proper seed & to make metalls   is required a metalline seed p 116 

 18, That though the stone may be made by resolving Metalls into their   principles yet that is no naturall dissolution. 

 19 That the corrosives & all heterogeneity must be parted from the stone Key 1 

 20 That the highest aurum potabile is made of the perfect Elixir before   fermentation. Test part 5 pag 3. 

 20 That an aurum potabile may be made of the 🜍  of common ☉   extracted with corrosives, & digested with 4 times as much spirit of ☿ , in which   digestion when the 🜍  begins to be dissolved & brought into its materia prima   there will appear at the edges round the glass a green circle then a blew   then a yellow, afterwards all the colours of the rainbow, which soon pass   away & when all is dissolved so that nothing remain in the bottom, put to it   twice as much well rectified spirit of wine & digest gently for 12 or 15   days more, then abstract per Alembicum & a blood red liquor comes over   Iterate the abstraction that nothing remains behind, so have you ☉  potabile   which is irreducible to a body.  22. The most part of ♀  body is a meer tincture. Keys pag 6.

Valentine s process described in his 12 Keys & other   writings    Purge ☉  with ♁  thrice, drive it of with   (Keys p 13, 14 & Testament p 120)   Amalgam it with 6 parts of ☿  vive, grind the amalgama with twice as much common 🜍 , evaporate it in a broad pan in a gentle heat under a muffle stirring   it with an iron rodd untill it become a calx of a marigold colour. But   let the fire be gentle that it melt not. (Test p 121, 151 )   Draw from prepared niter & salarmoniack ana (Test p 151) an aqua Regis   pour thrice as much of this on the said calx & digest them a day or two till the feces settle   (Test p 121 ) decant & in the remaining calx pour more   Menstruum etc: till all be dissolved into splendent transparent   yellow oyle. Digest this solution for 9 days or longer & when any   feces fall separate them. Abstract the water to a spissitute like an   oyle, & cohobate the abstracted water thrice or oftener. (Test p 121 152)   & Exper p 4, 19   Then Take spirit of Salniter 1 part & well dephlegmed spirit of common salt   3 parts pour these spirits alittle warmed on the affosaid oyle or calx   of ☉  from whence the former Menstruum was abstracted, digest it, abstract   to an oleity, & cohobate it often augmenting the fire every time til all the   gold come over. And then abstract in Balneo Mariae gently, & let it chrystalize   Test p 152, 153, 121, & 122.   Note 1 That this second water is stronger & less corrosive then the   first & may be used instead of it. 2 That all the cohobations may be   with a pretty strong heat to make as much gold rise as you can, & the   heat must be stronger in the last cohobations then in the first. 3   that both the Menstruums must be abstracted gently that none of the gold   rise with them. Test p 121, 122, 152 & 153.   Having chrystallized your gold dry the crystalls in a reverberatory pan   under a muffle with a graduated heat that all its corrosiveness be taken   away & the pouder will be of a fair scarlet colour Test p  1 22. Or   else dissolve the crystalls in distilld water, put to them 3 times as much   purged ☿ , shake it & many colours will appear & the amalgama falls to the   bottom. evaporate it gently under a muffle stirring it with a wier   & there will remain a purple or scarlet coloured subtile pouder. Test p 153.   & Experiments p 2 .   All this is to open the body of ☉  that it may part with its anima or 🜍  <11.10v> Page Image13    On this pouder pour fresh spirit of common salt mixt with prepared spirit   of wine & brought to a sweetness by digestion Test 122 & 153, digest till   the spirit be deeply tinged red, decant, pour on new spirit etc. till all the   tincture be drawn of, evaporate the abstractions gently in Balneo Mariae & you will   have the 🜍  of ☉  in a subtile red pouder which must be dulcified with distilld   rain water Test p 122, 153 154   Let the remaining Calx (which will be white like calx vive) be reverberated   half an hower gently that it became a corporeall fixt pouder. Then pour on it well   rectified hony-water, which is corrosive, to extract its salt which will be done   in 10 days. Abstract in Balneo Mariae edulcorate the 🜔  with common distilld water   & clarify it with spirit of wine Elucid p 129, 155.   Having throughly freed this sulphur & salt of ☉  from the  corrosives, digest the 🜔  with thrice, or perhaps 4 times as much spirit of ☿    Exper p 11. And in 40 days it will putrefy & after blacknes & colours coagulate into a new body 
  as white as snow Test p 136 145 146.   And when it hath been thrice digested to a white or Lunar fixation   put to it the 🜍  of ☉ , first digested to an oyle in twice or thrice as much spirit   of ☿ , & govern it to the red. Test p 127, 128, 129, , 137   Multiply this with spirit of ☿  Test p 130. And ferment it by   fusion with ☉  for 3 days. Keys p 38.
